```
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    28→
    29→    count: int | None = 1
    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    scope: str | None = None
    32→    status: str = "open"

    52→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    53→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    54→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    *,
    60→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    61→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    62→) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    64→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    65→        return items
    66→
    67→    subjective_items = deque(
    68→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    69→    )
    70→    objective_items = deque(
    71→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    72→    )
    73→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    74→        return items
    75→
    76→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    77→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    78→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    79→            if not objective_items:
    80→                break
    81→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    82→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    83→            if not subjective_items:
    84→                break
    85→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    86→
    87→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    88→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    89→    return interleaved
    90→
    91→
    92→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    93→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    94→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    95→    if not scan_history:
    96→        return set()
    97→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    98→    if not threshold:
    99→        return set()
   100→    return {
   101→        fid
   102→        for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   103→        if f.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
   104→    }
   105→
   106→
   107→def _apply_plan_order(
   108→    items: list[dict],
   109→    plan: dict,
   110→    *,
   111→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   112→    cluster: str | None = None,
   113→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   114→) -> list[dict]:
   115→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   116→
   117→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   118→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   119→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   120→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   121→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   122→    """
   123→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   124→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   125→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   126→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   127→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   128→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   129→
   130→    # Build lookup
   131→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   132→    for item in items:
   133→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   134→
   135→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   136→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   137→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   138→        if override.get("description"):
   139→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   140→        if override.get("note"):
   141→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   142→        if override.get("cluster"):
   143→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   144→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   145→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   146→                "name": cluster_name,
   147→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   148→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   149→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   150→            }
   151→
   152→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   153→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   154→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   155→    for fid in queue_order:
   156→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   157→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   158→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   159→
   160→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   161→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   162→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   163→    _new = new_ids or set()
   164→    for item in items:
   165→        item_id = item["id"]
   166→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   167→            continue
   168→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   169→            skipped_items.append(item)
   170→        elif item_id in _new:
   171→            remaining_new.append(item)
   172→        else:
   173→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   174→
   175→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   176→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   177→    if include_skipped:
   178→        result = result + skipped_items
   179→
   180→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   181→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   182→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   183→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   184→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   185→            if skip_entry:
   186→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   187→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   188→                if skip_reason:
   189→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   190→
   191→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   192→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   193→    if effective_cluster:
   194→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   195→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   196→        if cluster_member_ids:
   197→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   198→
   199→    return result
   200→
   201→
   202→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   203→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   204→    try:
   205→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   206→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   207→        if meta:
   208→            return meta.action_type
   209→    except ImportError as exc:
   210→        _ = exc
   211→    return "manual_fix"
   212→
   213→
   214→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   215→
   216→
   217→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   218→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   219→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   220→    if not clusters:
   221→        return items
   222→
   223→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   224→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   225→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   226→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   227→            continue
   228→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   229→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   230→
   231→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   232→        return items
   233→
   234→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   235→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   236→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   237→
   238→    for item in items:
   239→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   240→        if cname:
   241→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   242→        else:
   243→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   244→
   245→    # Build cluster meta-items
   246→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   247→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   248→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   249→        if len(members) < 2:
   250→            result.extend(members)
   251→            continue
   252→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   253→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   254→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   255→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   256→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   257→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   258→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   259→            action_type = "reorganize"
   260→        else:
   261→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   262→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   263→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   264→                action_type = "refactor"
   265→
   266→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   267→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   268→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   269→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   270→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   271→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   272→        else:
   273→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   274→
   275→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   276→        if not primary_command:
   277→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   278→
   279→        meta_item: dict = {
   280→            "id": cname,
   281→            "kind": "cluster",
   282→            "action_type": action_type,
   283→            "summary": summary,
   284→            "members": members,
   285→            "member_count": len(members),
   286→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   287→            "cluster_name": cname,
   288→            "cluster_auto": True,
   289→            "confidence": "high",
   290→            "detector": detector,
   291→            "file": "",
   292→        }
   293→        result.append(meta_item)
   294→
   295→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   296→    return result
   297→
   298→
   299→def build_work_queue(
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   303→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   304→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   305→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   306→
   307→    status = resolved_options.status
   308→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   309→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   310→    try:
   311→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   312→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   313→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   314→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   315→
   316→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   317→        state,
   318→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   319→        status_filter=status,
   320→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   321→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   322→    )
   323→
   324→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   325→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   326→    suppressed_count = 0
   327→    if (
   328→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   329→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   330→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   331→    ):
   332→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   333→            state,
   334→            state.get("findings", {}),
   335→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   336→        )
   337→        for item in subjective_items:
   338→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   339→                continue
   340→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   341→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   342→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   343→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   344→                suppressed_count += 1
   345→                continue
   346→            all_items.append(item)
   347→
   348→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   349→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   350→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   351→        if synth_item is not None:
   352→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   353→
   354→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   355→
   356→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   357→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   358→    if resolved_options.plan:
   359→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   360→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   361→            all_items,
   362→            resolved_options.plan,
   363→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   364→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   365→            new_ids=new_ids,
   366→        )
   367→
   368→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   369→    should_collapse = (
   370→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   371→        and resolved_options.plan
   372→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   373→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   374→    )
   375→    if should_collapse:
   376→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   377→
   378→    should_interleave = (
   379→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   380→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   381→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   382→        and not resolved_options.scope
   383→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   384→    )
   385→    if should_interleave:
   386→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   387→
   388→    total = len(all_items)
   389→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   390→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   391→
   392→    if resolved_options.explain:
   393→        for item in all_items:
   394→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   395→
   396→    return {
   397→        "items": all_items,
   398→        "total": total,
   399→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   400→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   401→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   402→    }
   403→
   404→

   407→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   408→    "WorkQueueResult",
   409→    "build_work_queue",
   410→    "group_queue_items",
   411→]
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   169→
   170→
   171→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   172→    """Build a synthetic T1 work item for ``synthesis::pending`` if it's in the queue.
   173→
   174→    Returns ``None`` when synthesis is not pending.
   175→    """
   176→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   177→
   178→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   182→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   183→
   184→    open_review_ids = {
   185→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   186→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   187→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   188→    }
   189→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   190→    new_since = len(open_review_ids - synthesized_ids)
   191→    resolved_since = len(synthesized_ids - open_review_ids)
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   195→        "tier": 1,
   196→        "confidence": "high",
   197→        "detector": "synthesis",
   198→        "file": ".",
   199→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   200→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   201→        "summary": (
   202→            f"Synthesize {len(open_review_ids)} review findings into a coherent plan"
   203→        ),
   204→        "detail": {
   205→            "total_review_findings": len(open_review_ids),
   206→            "new_since_last": new_since,
   207→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since,
   208→        },
   209→    }
   210→
   211→
   212→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   213→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   214→    if not dim_scores:
   215→        return {}
   216→
   217→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   218→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   219→    for entry in entries:
   220→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   221→        if not name:
   222→            continue
   223→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   224→        [REDACTED](name)
   225→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   226→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   227→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   228→            if not key:
   229→                continue
   230→            aliases.add(key)
   231→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   232→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   233→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   234→        for alias in aliases:
   235→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   236→    return scores
   237→
   238→
   239→def build_subjective_items(
   240→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   241→) -> list[dict]:
   242→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   243→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   244→    if not dim_scores:
   245→        return []
   246→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   247→
   248→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   249→    if not subjective_entries:
   250→        return []
   251→    unassessed_dims = {
   252→        str(name).strip()
   253→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   254→            dim_scores
   255→        )
   256→    }
   257→
   258→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   259→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   260→    for finding in findings.values():
   261→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   262→            continue
   263→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   264→        if not dim_key:
   265→            continue
   266→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   267→
   268→    items: list[dict] = []
   269→    def _prepare_command(
   270→        cli_keys: list[str],
   271→        *,
   272→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   273→    ) -> str:
   274→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   275→        if cli_keys:
   276→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   277→        if force_review_rerun:
   278→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   279→        return command
   280→
   281→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   282→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   283→        if not name:
   284→            continue
   285→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   286→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   287→            continue
   288→
   289→        [REDACTED](name)
   290→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   291→        cli_keys = [
   292→            str(key).strip().lower()
   293→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   294→            if str(key).strip()
   295→        ]
   296→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   297→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   298→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   299→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   300→            name in unassessed_dims
   301→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   302→        )
   303→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   304→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   305→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   306→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   307→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   308→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   309→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   310→        if open_review > 0:
   311→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   312→        elif is_unassessed:
   313→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   314→        elif is_stale:
   315→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   316→                cli_keys,
   317→                force_review_rerun=True,
   318→            )
   319→        elif cli_keys:
   320→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   321→                cli_keys,
   322→                force_review_rerun=True,
   323→            )
   324→        else:
   325→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   326→                cli_keys,
   327→                force_review_rerun=True,
   328→            )
   329→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   330→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   331→        items.append(
   332→            {
   333→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   334→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   335→                "file": ".",
   336→                "confidence": "medium",
   337→                "summary": summary,
   338→                "detail": {
   339→                    "dimension_name": name,
   340→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   341→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   342→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   343→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   344→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   345→                },
   346→                "status": "open",
   347→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   348→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   349→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   350→            }
   351→        )
   352→    return items
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   357→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   358→    "build_subjective_items",
   359→    "build_synthesis_item",
   360→    "detail_dict",
   361→    "is_review_finding",
   362→    "is_subjective_finding",
   363→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   364→    "review_finding_weight",
   365→    "scope_matches",
   366→    "slugify",
   367→    "status_matches",
   368→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   369→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   370→]
   371→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["review_weight"] = (
    56→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    57→        )
    58→        subjective_score = None
    59→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    60→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    61→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    62→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    63→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    64→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    65→            )
    66→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    67→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    68→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    69→            item,
    70→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    71→        )
    72→
    73→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    74→            continue
    75→        out.append(item)
    76→
    77→    return out
    78→
    79→
    80→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
    85→        # Synthesis items always sort first — they block all other work.
    86→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    89→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    90→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    91→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    92→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    93→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    94→        )
    95→        return (
    96→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
    97→            action_pri,
    98→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
    99→            item.get("id", ""),
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   103→        return (
   104→            1,
   105→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   106→            subjective_score_value(item),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   111→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   112→    return (
   113→        1,
   114→        0,
   115→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   116→        -review_weight,
   117→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   118→        item.get("id", ""),
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   123→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   124→        return {
   125→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   126→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   127→            "policy": (
   128→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   129→            ),
   130→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   131→        }
   132→
   133→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   134→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   135→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   136→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   137→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   138→    if is_subjective:
   139→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   140→    elif is_review:
   141→        ranking_factors = [
   142→            "confidence asc",
   143→            "review_weight desc",
   144→            "count desc",
   145→            "id asc",
   146→        ]
   147→    else:
   148→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   149→    explain = {
   150→        "kind": "finding",
   151→        "confidence": confidence,
   152→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   153→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   154→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   155→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   156→    }
   157→    if is_review:
   158→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   159→    if is_subjective:
   160→        explain["policy"] = (
   161→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   162→        )
   163→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   164→    return explain
   165→
   166→
   167→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   168→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   169→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   170→    for item in items:
   171→        if group == "file":
   172→            key = item.get("file", "")
   173→        elif group == "detector":
   174→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   175→        elif group == "cluster":
   176→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   177→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   178→        else:
   179→            key = "items"
   180→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   181→    return grouped
   182→
   183→
   184→__all__ = [
   185→    "build_finding_items",
   186→    "item_explain",
   187→    "item_sort_key",
   188→    "subjective_score_value",
   189→    "group_queue_items",
   190→]
   191→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   105→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   106→
   107→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   108→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   109→    """
   110→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   111→    if not promoted:
   112→        return 0
   113→    last_idx = -1
   114→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   115→        if fid in promoted:
   116→            last_idx = i
   117→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   118→
   119→
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   122→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→
   124→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   128→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   129→
   130→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   131→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   132→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   133→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   134→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   135→    """
   136→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   137→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   138→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   139→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   140→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   141→
   142→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   143→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   144→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   145→        fid for fid in order
   146→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   147→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   148→        and fid not in stale_ids
   149→    ]
   150→    for fid in to_remove:
   151→        order.remove(fid)
   152→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   153→
   154→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   155→    existing = set(order)
   156→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   157→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   158→        if uid not in existing:
   159→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   160→            result.injected.append(uid)
   161→
   162→    return result
   163→
   164→
   165→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   167→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→
   169→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   170→    plan: PlanModel,
   171→    state: StateModel,
   172→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   173→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   174→
   175→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   176→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   177→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   178→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   179→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   180→    """
   181→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   182→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   183→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   184→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in stale_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   200→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   201→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   202→        existing = set(order)
   203→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   204→            if sid not in existing:
   205→                order.append(sid)
   206→                result.injected.append(sid)
   207→
   208→    return result
   209→
   210→
   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→
   215→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   216→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   217→
   218→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   219→    """
   220→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   221→    review_ids = sorted(
   222→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   223→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   224→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   225→    )
   226→    if not review_ids:
   227→        return ""
   228→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   229→
   230→
   231→@dataclass
   232→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   233→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   234→
   235→    injected: bool = False
   236→    pruned: bool = False
   237→
   238→    @property
   239→    def changes(self) -> int:
   240→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   241→
   242→
   243→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   244→    plan: PlanModel,
   245→    state: StateModel,
   246→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   247→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   248→
   249→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   250→    """
   251→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   252→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   253→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   254→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   255→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   256→
   257→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   258→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   259→
   260→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject after promoted items
   261→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   262→        order.insert(_after_promoted(order, plan), SYNTHESIS_ID)
   263→        result.injected = True
   264→
   265→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   266→
   267→    return result
   268→
   269→
   270→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   271→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   272→
   273→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   274→    """
   275→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   276→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   277→    current = {
   278→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   279→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   280→    }
   281→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   282→
   283→
   284→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   285→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   286→
   287→    Returns True when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue OR the
   288→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   289→    """
   290→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   291→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   292→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   293→        return True
   294→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   295→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   296→    if not last_hash:
   297→        return False
   298→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   299→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   300→
   301→
   302→__all__ = [
   303→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   304→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   305→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   306→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   307→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   308→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   309→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   310→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   311→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   312→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   313→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   314→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   315→]
   316→
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    79→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    80→
    81→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    82→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    84→        if subtype:
    85→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    86→
    87→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    88→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    89→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    90→
    91→    # Everything else → by detector
    92→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    93→
    94→
    95→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    96→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    97→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    98→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    99→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   100→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   101→    # detector::security → auto/security
   102→    parts = key.split("::")
   103→    if len(parts) == 2:
   104→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   105→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   106→        if prefix_type == "review":
   107→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   108→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   109→    if len(parts) == 3:
   110→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   111→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   112→
   113→
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# Description and action generation
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→def _generate_description(
   119→    cluster_name: str,
   120→    members: list[dict],
   121→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   122→    subtype: str | None,
   123→) -> str:
   124→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   125→    count = len(members)
   126→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   127→
   128→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   129→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   130→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   131→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   132→
   133→    if detector == "structural":
   134→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   135→        if len(files) == 1:
   136→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   137→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   138→
   139→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   140→    if subtype:
   141→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   142→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   143→
   144→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   145→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   146→
   147→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   148→
   149→
   150→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   151→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   152→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   153→        return None
   154→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   155→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   156→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   157→        return fixer_name
   158→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   159→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   160→        if subtype in fixer:
   161→            return fixer
   162→    return None
   163→
   164→
   165→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   166→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   167→
   168→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   169→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   170→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   171→    """
   172→    if " — " in guidance:
   173→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   174→    return guidance
   175→
   176→
   177→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   178→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   179→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   180→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   181→}
   182→
   183→
   184→def _generate_action(
   185→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   186→    subtype: str | None,
   187→) -> str:
   188→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   189→
   190→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   191→    """
   192→    if meta is None:
   193→        return "review and fix each finding"
   194→
   195→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   196→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   197→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   198→        if matched_fixer:
   199→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   200→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   201→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   202→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   203→
   204→    if meta.tool == "move":
   205→        return "desloppify move"
   206→
   207→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   208→    if meta.guidance:
   209→        if subtype:
   210→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   211→        return meta.guidance
   212→
   213→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   214→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   215→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   216→    )
   217→
   218→
   219→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   220→# Repair
   221→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   222→
   223→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
   224→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
   225→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   226→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   227→    repaired = 0
   228→    for override in overrides.values():
   229→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
   230→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
   231→            override["cluster"] = None
   232→            override["updated_at"] = now
   233→            repaired += 1
   234→    return repaired
   235→
   236→
   237→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   238→# Main algorithm
   239→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   240→
   241→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   242→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   243→
   244→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   245→    """
   246→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   247→    changes = 0
   248→
   249→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   250→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   251→
   252→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   253→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   254→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   255→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   256→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   257→
   258→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   259→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   260→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   261→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   262→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   263→            continue
   264→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   265→            continue
   266→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   267→            continue
   268→
   269→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   270→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   271→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   272→        if key is None:
   273→            continue
   274→
   275→        groups[key].append(fid)
   276→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   277→
   278→    # Drop singleton groups
   279→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   280→
   281→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   282→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   283→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   284→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   285→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   286→            if ck:
   287→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   288→
   289→    now = utc_now()
   290→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   291→
   292→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   293→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   294→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   295→
   296→        # Representative finding for metadata
   297→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   298→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   299→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   300→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   301→
   302→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   303→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   304→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   305→
   306→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   307→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   308→
   309→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   310→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   311→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   312→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   313→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   314→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   315→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   316→                if new_ids:
   317→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   318→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   319→                    changes += 1
   320→            else:
   321→                # Replace membership wholesale
   322→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   323→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   324→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   325→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   326→                    cluster["description"] = description
   327→                    cluster["action"] = action
   328→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   329→                    changes += 1
   330→        else:
   331→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   332→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   333→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   334→                # Append a disambiguator
   335→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   336→
   337→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   338→                "name": cluster_name,
   339→                "description": description,
   340→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   341→                "created_at": now,
   342→                "updated_at": now,
   343→                "auto": True,
   344→                "cluster_key": key,
   345→                "action": action,
   346→                "user_modified": False,
   347→            }
   348→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   349→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   350→            changes += 1
   351→
   352→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   353→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   354→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   355→        for fid in member_ids:
   356→            if fid not in overrides:
   357→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   358→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   359→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   360→
   361→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   362→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   363→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   364→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   365→    )
   366→    unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   367→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   368→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   369→    )
   370→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   371→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   372→    )
   373→
   374→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   375→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   376→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   377→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   378→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   379→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   380→
   381→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   382→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   383→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   384→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   385→            new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
   386→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   387→                cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
   388→                cluster["description"] = description
   389→                cluster["action"] = action
   390→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   391→                changes += 1
   392→        else:
   393→            clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
   394→                "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
   395→                "description": description,
   396→                "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
   397→                "created_at": now,
   398→                "updated_at": now,
   399→                "auto": True,
   400→                "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
   401→                "action": action,
   402→                "user_modified": False,
   403→            }
   404→            existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
   405→            changes += 1
   406→
   407→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   408→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
   409→        for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
   410→            if fid not in overrides:
   411→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   412→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   413→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   414→
   415→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   416→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   417→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   418→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   419→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   420→        action = (
   421→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   422→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   423→            + " --force-review-rerun"
   424→        )
   425→
   426→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   427→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   428→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   429→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   430→            new_ids = set(stale_queue_ids)
   431→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   432→                cluster["finding_ids"] = stale_queue_ids
   433→                cluster["description"] = description
   434→                cluster["action"] = action
   435→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   436→                changes += 1
   437→        else:
   438→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   439→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   440→                "description": description,
   441→                "finding_ids": stale_queue_ids,
   442→                "created_at": now,
   443→                "updated_at": now,
   444→                "auto": True,
   445→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   446→                "action": action,
   447→                "user_modified": False,
   448→            }
   449→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   450→            changes += 1
   451→
   452→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   453→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   454→        for fid in stale_queue_ids:
   455→            if fid not in overrides:
   456→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   457→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   458→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   459→
   460→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   461→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   462→        cluster = clusters[name]
   463→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   464→            continue
   465→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   466→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   467→            continue
   468→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   469→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   470→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   471→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   472→            if alive:
   473→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   474→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   475→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   476→                    changes += 1
   477→                continue
   478→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   479→        # Delete stale cluster
   480→        del clusters[name]
   481→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   482→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   483→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   484→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   485→                override["cluster"] = None
   486→                override["updated_at"] = now
   487→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   488→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   489→        changes += 1
   490→
   491→    # Repair any orphaned override cluster references from prior bugs
   492→    repaired = _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   493→    changes += repaired
   494→
   495→    plan["updated"] = now
   496→    return changes
   497→
   498→
   499→__all__ = [
   500→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   501→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   502→]
   503→
```
