1→"""Dimension analysis and debt computation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import STRUCTURAL_MERGE
     6→from desloppify.scoring import (
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    TIER_WEIGHTS,
     9→    compute_score_impact,
    10→    merge_potentials,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.state import StateModel, path_scoped_findings
    13→
    14→
    15→def _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict], state: StateModel) -> dict:
    16→    """Compute per-dimension structured analysis."""
    17→    if not dim_scores:
    18→        return {}
    19→
    20→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    21→    return {
    22→        "lowest_dimensions": _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores, potentials),
    23→        "biggest_gap_dimensions": _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores, state)[:3],
    24→        "stagnant_dimensions": _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores, history),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict) -> list[dict]:
    29→    """Build summary entries for the lowest strict-scoring dimensions."""
    30→    sorted_dims = sorted(
    31→        (
    32→            (name, ds)
    33→            for name, ds in dim_scores.items()
    34→            if ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) < 100
    35→        ),
    36→        key=lambda item: item[1].get("strict", item[1]["score"]),
    37→    )
    38→
    39→    lowest = []
    40→    for name, ds in sorted_dims[:3]:
    41→        strict = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    42→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
    43→        impact = _dominant_detector_impact(
    44→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    45→            detectors=ds.get("detectors", {}),
    46→            potentials=potentials,
    47→        )
    48→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in ds.get("detectors", {})
    49→        entry = {
    50→            "name": name,
    51→            "strict": round(strict, 1),
    52→            "issues": issues,
    53→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
    54→        }
    55→        if is_subjective:
    56→            entry["subjective"] = True
    57→            entry["impact_description"] = "re-review to improve"
    58→        lowest.append(entry)
    59→    return lowest
    60→
    61→
    62→def _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Build summary entries for dimensions with the biggest strict gap."""
    64→    biggest_gap = []
    65→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(
    66→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
    67→    )
    68→    for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
    69→        lenient = ds["score"]
    70→        strict = ds.get("strict", lenient)
    71→        gap = lenient - strict
    72→        if gap > 1.0:
    73→            wontfix_count = sum(
    74→                1
    75→                for f in scoped.values()
    76→                if f["status"] == "wontfix"
    77→                and _finding_in_dimension(f, name, dim_scores)
    78→            )
    79→            biggest_gap.append(
    80→                {
    81→                    "name": name,
    82→                    "lenient": round(lenient, 1),
    83→                    "strict": round(strict, 1),
    84→                    "gap": round(gap, 1),
    85→                    "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
    86→                }
    87→            )
    88→    biggest_gap.sort(key=lambda x: -x["gap"])
    89→    return biggest_gap
    90→
    91→
    92→def _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    93→    """Build summary entries for dimensions that have not moved recently."""
    94→    if len(history) < 3:
    95→        return []
    96→
    97→    stagnant = []
    98→    for name in dim_scores:
    99→        scores = _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name, history)
   100→        if len(scores) < 3 or max(scores) - min(scores) > 0.5:
   101→            continue
   102→        stagnant.append(
   103→            {
   104→                "name": name,
   105→                "strict": round(dim_scores[name].get("strict", dim_scores[name]["score"]), 1),
   106→                "stuck_scans": len(scores),
   107→            }
   108→        )
   109→    return stagnant
   110→
   111→
   112→def _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name: str, history: list[dict]) -> list[float]:
   113→    """Collect recent strict score samples for one dimension."""
   114→    scores: list[float] = []
   115→    for history_entry in history[-5:]:
   116→        dim_entry = (history_entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(name)
   117→        if not dim_entry:
   118→            continue
   119→        strict_value = dim_entry.get("strict", dim_entry.get("score"))
   120→        if strict_value is not None:
   121→            scores.append(float(strict_value))
   122→    return scores
   123→
   124→
   125→def _dominant_detector_impact(
   126→    *,
   127→    dim_scores: dict,
   128→    detectors: dict,
   129→    potentials: dict,
   130→) -> float:
   131→    """Estimate impact using the most consequential detector in a dimension."""
   132→    normalized_scores = {
   133→        key: {
   134→            "score": value["score"],
   135→            "tier": value.get("tier", 3),
   136→            "detectors": value.get("detectors", {}),
   137→        }
   138→        for key, value in dim_scores.items()
   139→    }
   140→    impact = 0.0
   141→    for detector_name, detector_data in detectors.items():
   142→        issue_count = int(detector_data.get("issues", 0) or 0)
   143→        if issue_count <= 0:
   144→            continue
   145→        detector_impact = compute_score_impact(
   146→            normalized_scores,
   147→            potentials,
   148→            detector_name,
   149→            issue_count,
   150→        )
   151→        impact = max(impact, detector_impact)
   152→    return impact
   153→
   154→
   155→def _finding_in_dimension(finding: dict, dim_name: str, dim_scores: dict) -> bool:
   156→    """Check if a finding's detector belongs to a dimension."""
   157→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   158→    if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   159→        detector = "structural"
   160→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   161→        if dim.name == dim_name and detector in dim.detectors:
   162→            return True
   163→    return False
   164→
   165→
   166→def _analyze_debt(dim_scores: dict, findings: dict, history: list[dict]) -> dict:
   167→    """Compute wontfix debt analysis."""
   168→    # Count wontfix
   169→    wontfix_count = sum(1 for f in findings.values() if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   170→
   171→    # Compute gap per dimension
   172→    worst_dim = None
   173→    worst_gap = 0.0
   174→    overall_lenient = 0.0
   175→    overall_strict = 0.0
   176→    if dim_scores:
   177→        w_sum_l = 0.0
   178→        w_sum_s = 0.0
   179→        w_total = 0.0
   180→        for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
   181→            tier = ds.get("tier", 3)
   182→            w = TIER_WEIGHTS.get(tier, 2)
   183→            w_sum_l += ds["score"] * w
   184→            w_sum_s += ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) * w
   185→            w_total += w
   186→            gap = ds["score"] - ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   187→            if gap > worst_gap:
   188→                worst_gap = gap
   189→                worst_dim = name
   190→        if w_total > 0:
   191→            overall_lenient = round(w_sum_l / w_total, 1)
   192→            overall_strict = round(w_sum_s / w_total, 1)
   193→
   194→    overall_gap = round(overall_lenient - overall_strict, 1)
   195→
   196→    # Trend from history
   197→    trend = "stable"
   198→    if len(history) >= 3:
   199→        gaps = []
   200→        for h in history[-5:]:
   201→            hs = h.get("strict_score")
   202→            hl = h.get("overall_score")
   203→            if hs is not None and hl is not None:
   204→                gaps.append(hl - hs)
   205→        if len(gaps) >= 2:
   206→            if gaps[-1] > gaps[0] + 0.5:
   207→                trend = "growing"
   208→            elif gaps[-1] < gaps[0] - 0.5:
   209→                trend = "shrinking"
   210→
   211→    return {
   212→        "overall_gap": overall_gap,
   213→        "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
   214→        "worst_dimension": worst_dim,
   215→        "worst_gap": round(worst_gap, 1),
   216→        "trend": trend,
   217→    }
   218→

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    QueueBuildOptions,
    10→    WorkQueueResult,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    count: int | None = None
    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    include_subjective: bool = True
    32→
    33→
    34→def build_open_plan_queue(
    35→    state: dict[str, Any],
    36→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    39→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    40→    resolved_scan_path: str | None
    41→    if isinstance(policy.scan_path, str):
    42→        resolved_scan_path = policy.scan_path
    43→    else:
    44→        raw_state_path = state.get("scan_path")
    45→        resolved_scan_path = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, str) else None
    46→    return build_work_queue(
    47→        state,
    48→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    49→            count=policy.count,
    50→            scan_path=resolved_scan_path,
    51→            status="open",
    52→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    53→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    54→        ),
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    59→

    87→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
    88→    return "_".join(str(name).strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    89→
    90→
    91→def _title_display_name(dimension_key: str) -> str:
    92→    return dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
    93→
    94→
    95→def _normalize_lang_name(lang_name: str | None) -> str | None:
    96→    if not isinstance(lang_name, str):
    97→        return None
    98→    cleaned = lang_name.strip().lower()
    99→    return cleaned or None
   100→
   101→
   102→def _extract_prompt_meta(entry: object) -> dict[str, object]:
   103→    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   104→        return {}
   105→    meta = entry.get("meta")
   106→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   107→        return {}
   108→    out: dict[str, object] = {}
   109→
   110→    if isinstance(meta.get("display_name"), str) and meta["display_name"].strip():
   111→        out["display_name"] = meta["display_name"].strip()
   112→
   113→    weight = meta.get("weight")
   114→    if is_numeric(weight):
   115→        out["weight"] = max(0.0, float(weight))
   116→
   117→    enabled = meta.get("enabled_by_default")
   118→    if isinstance(enabled, bool):
   119→        out["enabled_by_default"] = enabled
   120→
   121→    reset_on_scan = meta.get("reset_on_scan")
   122→    if isinstance(reset_on_scan, bool):
   123→        out["reset_on_scan"] = reset_on_scan
   124→
   125→    return out
   126→
   127→
   128→def _merge_prompt_display_and_weights(
   129→    payload: dict[str, object],
   130→    *,
   131→    prompt_meta: dict[str, object],
   132→    override_existing: bool,
   133→) -> None:
   134→    if "display_name" in prompt_meta and (
   135→        override_existing or "display_name" not in payload
   136→    ):
   137→        payload["display_name"] = prompt_meta["display_name"]
   138→    if "weight" in prompt_meta and (override_existing or "weight" not in payload):
   139→        payload["weight"] = prompt_meta["weight"]
   140→    if "reset_on_scan" in prompt_meta and (
   141→        override_existing or "reset_on_scan" not in payload
   142→    ):
   143→        payload["reset_on_scan"] = prompt_meta["reset_on_scan"]
   144→
   145→
   146→def _merge_enabled_by_default_flag(
   147→    payload: dict[str, object],
   148→    *,
   149→    prompt_meta: dict[str, object],
   150→    override_existing: bool,
   151→    default_enabled: bool,
   152→) -> None:
   153→    if default_enabled:
   154→        payload["enabled_by_default"] = True
   155→    if "enabled_by_default" not in prompt_meta:
   156→        return
   157→    prompt_enabled = bool(prompt_meta["enabled_by_default"])
   158→    if override_existing:
   159→        payload["enabled_by_default"] = prompt_enabled
   160→        return
   161→    payload["enabled_by_default"] = bool(
   162→        payload.get("enabled_by_default", False) or prompt_enabled
   163→    )
   164→
   165→
   166→def _normalized_default_dimensions(dimensions: list[str]) -> set[str]:
   167→    return {
   168→        _normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   169→        for dim in dimensions
   170→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   171→    }
   172→
   173→
   174→def _merge_dimension_meta(
   175→    target: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   176→    *,
   177→    dimensions: list[str],
   178→    prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   179→    override_existing: bool = False,
   180→) -> None:
   181→    defaults = _normalized_default_dimensions(dimensions)
   182→
   183→    for raw_dim, entry in prompts.items():
   184→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw_dim)
   185→        if not dim:
   186→            continue
   187→
   188→        payload = target.setdefault(dim, {})
   189→        prompt_meta = _extract_prompt_meta(entry)
   190→        _merge_prompt_display_and_weights(
   191→            payload,
   192→            prompt_meta=prompt_meta,
   193→            override_existing=override_existing,
   194→        )
   195→        _merge_enabled_by_default_flag(
   196→            payload,
   197→            prompt_meta=prompt_meta,
   198→            override_existing=override_existing,
   199→            default_enabled=dim in defaults,
   200→        )
   201→
   202→
   203→def _default_available_languages() -> list[str]:
   204→    try:
   205→        return list(_available_langs())
   206→    except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError):
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→
   210→def _default_load_dimensions_payload() -> tuple[
   211→    list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str
   212→]:
   213→    return _load_dimensions()
   214→
   215→
   216→def _default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(
   217→    lang_name: str,
   218→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   219→    return _load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   220→
   221→
   222→class _SubjectiveProviderState:
   223→    def __init__(self) -> None:
   224→        self.available_languages_provider: Callable[[], list[str]] = (
   225→            _default_available_languages
   226→        )
   227→        self.load_dimensions_payload_provider: Callable[
   228→            [], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   229→        ] = _default_load_dimensions_payload
   230→        self.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider: Callable[
   231→            [str], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   232→        ] = _default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang
   233→
   234→
   235→_PROVIDER_STATE = _SubjectiveProviderState()
   236→
   237→
   238→def _available_languages() -> list[str]:
   239→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.available_languages_provider()
   240→
   241→
   242→def _load_dimensions_payload() -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   243→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.load_dimensions_payload_provider()
   244→
   245→
   246→def _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(
   247→    lang_name: str,
   248→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   249→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider(lang_name)
   250→
   251→
   252→def _clear_subjective_dimension_caches() -> None:
   253→    default_dimension_keys.cache_clear()
   254→    default_dimension_keys_for_lang.cache_clear()
   255→    load_subjective_dimension_metadata.cache_clear()
   256→    load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang.cache_clear()
   257→
   258→
   259→def configure_subjective_dimension_providers(
   260→    *,
   261→    available_languages_provider: Callable[[], list[str]] | None = None,
   262→    load_dimensions_payload_provider: Callable[
   263→        [], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   264→    ]
   265→    | None = None,
   266→    load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider: Callable[
   267→        [str], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   268→    ]
   269→    | None = None,
   270→) -> None:
   271→    """Configure metadata providers for subjective-dimension lookups."""
   272→    state = _PROVIDER_STATE
   273→
   274→    changed = False
   275→    if (
   276→        available_languages_provider is not None
   277→        and available_languages_provider is not state.available_languages_provider
   278→    ):
   279→        state.available_languages_provider = available_languages_provider
   280→        changed = True
   281→    if (
   282→        load_dimensions_payload_provider is not None
   283→        and load_dimensions_payload_provider
   284→        is not state.load_dimensions_payload_provider
   285→    ):
   286→        state.load_dimensions_payload_provider = load_dimensions_payload_provider
   287→        changed = True
   288→    if (
   289→        load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider is not None
   290→        and load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   291→        is not state.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   292→    ):
   293→        state.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider = (
   294→            load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   295→        )
   296→        changed = True
   297→
   298→    if changed:
   299→        _clear_subjective_dimension_caches()
   300→
   301→
   302→def reset_subjective_dimension_providers() -> None:
   303→    """Reset metadata providers to built-in defaults."""
   304→    configure_subjective_dimension_providers(
   305→        available_languages_provider=_default_available_languages,
   306→        load_dimensions_payload_provider=_default_load_dimensions_payload,
   307→        load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider=_default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang,
   308→    )
   309→
   310→
   311→def _normalize_dimension_list(values: list[str]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   312→    normalized: list[str] = []
   313→    for raw in values:
   314→        if not isinstance(raw, str):
   315→            continue
   316→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw)
   317→        if not dim or dim in normalized:
   318→            continue
   319→        normalized.append(dim)
   320→    return tuple(normalized)
   321→
   322→
   323→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   324→def default_dimension_keys() -> tuple[str, ...]:
   325→    """Return canonical default subjective dimension keys."""
   326→    try:
   327→        dims, _, _ = _load_dimensions_payload()
   328→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   329→        logger.debug("Failed to load default subjective dimensions: %s", exc)
   330→        return tuple(_LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.keys())
   331→    return _normalize_dimension_list(dims)
   332→
   333→
   334→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   335→def default_dimension_keys_for_lang(lang_name: str | None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   336→    """Return default subjective dimension keys for a specific language."""
   337→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   338→    if normalized is None:
   339→        return default_dimension_keys()
   340→    try:
   341→        dims, _, _ = _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(normalized)
   342→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   343→        logger.debug(
   344→            "Failed to load subjective dimensions for lang %s: %s",
   345→            normalized,
   346→            exc,
   347→        )
   348→        return default_dimension_keys()
   349→    return _normalize_dimension_list(dims)
   350→
   351→
   352→def _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(
   353→    *,
   354→    lang_name: str | None,
   355→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   356→    out: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   357→
   358→    try:
   359→        shared_defaults, shared_prompts, _ = _load_dimensions_payload()
   360→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   361→        logger.debug("Failed to load shared subjective dimension payload: %s", exc)
   362→        shared_defaults, shared_prompts = [], {}
   363→    _merge_dimension_meta(out, dimensions=shared_defaults, prompts=shared_prompts)
   364→
   365→    langs = (
   366→        [lang_name]
   367→        if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip()
   368→        else _available_languages()
   369→    )
   370→    for name in langs:
   371→        try:
   372→            lang_defaults, lang_prompts, _ = _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(name)
   373→            _merge_dimension_meta(
   374→                out,
   375→                dimensions=lang_defaults,
   376→                prompts=lang_prompts,
   377→                override_existing=bool(lang_name),
   378→            )
   379→        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   380→            logger.debug("Failed to load dimensions for lang %s: %s", name, exc)
   381→            continue
   382→
   383→    for dim, payload in out.items():
   384→        payload.setdefault(
   385→            "display_name",
   386→            _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim, _title_display_name(dim)),
   387→        )
   388→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   389→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   390→        if dim in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES:
   391→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   392→        else:
   393→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   394→
   395→    # Preserve legacy dimensions even if a payload temporarily drops one.
   396→    for dim, display in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   397→        payload = out.setdefault(dim, {})
   398→        payload.setdefault("display_name", display)
   399→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   400→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", True)
   401→        payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   402→
   403→    return out
   404→
   405→
   406→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   407→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   408→    """Return merged metadata across all known dimensions/languages."""
   409→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=None)
   410→
   411→
   412→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   413→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(
   414→    lang_name: str | None,
   415→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   416→    """Return merged metadata for one language (with language overrides)."""
   417→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   418→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=normalized)
   419→
   420→
   421→def _metadata_registry(lang_name: str | None) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   422→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   423→    if normalized is None:
   424→        return load_subjective_dimension_metadata()
   425→    return load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(normalized)
   426→
   427→
   428→def get_dimension_metadata(
   429→    dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None
   430→) -> dict[str, object]:
   431→    """Return metadata for one dimension key (with sane defaults)."""
   432→    dim = _normalize_dimension_name(dimension_name)
   433→    all_meta = _metadata_registry(lang_name)
   434→    payload = dict(all_meta.get(dim, {}))
   435→
   436→    payload.setdefault("display_name", _title_display_name(dim))
   437→    payload.setdefault("weight", 1.0)
   438→    payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   439→    payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   440→    return payload
   441→
   442→
   443→def dimension_display_name(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> str:
   444→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   445→    return str(meta.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dimension_name)))
   446→
   447→
   448→def dimension_weight(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> float:
   449→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   450→    raw = meta.get("weight", 1.0)
   451→    if is_numeric(raw):
   452→        return max(0.0, float(raw))
   453→    return 1.0
   454→
   455→
   456→def default_display_names_map(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
   457→    """Display-name map for default subjective dimensions."""
   458→    out: dict[str, str] = {}
   459→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   460→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   461→            continue
   462→        out[dim] = str(payload.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dim)))
   463→    return out
   464→
   465→
   466→def resettable_default_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   467→    """Default subjective dimensions that should be reset by scan reset."""
   468→    out = []
   469→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   470→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   471→            continue
   472→        if not bool(payload.get("reset_on_scan", True)):
   473→            continue
   474→        out.append(dim)
   475→    return tuple(sorted(set(out)))
   476→
   477→
   478→__all__ = [
   479→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   480→    "configure_subjective_dimension_providers",
   481→    "default_dimension_keys",
   482→    "default_dimension_keys_for_lang",
   483→    "default_display_names_map",
   484→    "dimension_display_name",
   485→    "dimension_weight",
   486→    "get_dimension_metadata",
   487→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata",
   488→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang",
   489→    "reset_subjective_dimension_providers",
   490→    "resettable_default_dimensions",
   491→]
   492→

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]

1→"""Lightweight integrity helpers for subjective/review scoring.
     2→
     3→This module intentionally lives outside ``desloppify.intelligence.review`` so command/state
     4→paths can import it without loading the heavier review package.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
    10→
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
    13→    matches_target_score,
    14→)
    15→
    16→__all__ = [
    17→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
    18→    "is_holistic_subjective_finding",
    19→    "is_subjective_review_open",
    20→    "matches_target_score",
    21→    "subjective_review_open_breakdown",
    22→    "unassessed_subjective_dimensions",
    23→]
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# Internal iteration helper
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→
    31→def _iter_findings(
    32→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    33→) -> Iterable[tuple[str, dict]]:
    34→    """Yield (finding_id, finding) pairs from mapping or iterable inputs."""
    35→    if isinstance(findings, Mapping):
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
    37→            if isinstance(finding, dict):
    38→                yield str(finding_id), finding
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    for index, finding in enumerate(findings):
    42→        if isinstance(finding, dict):
    43→            yield str(index), finding
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Public helpers (formerly in integrity/review.py)
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→
    51→def is_subjective_review_open(finding: dict) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True when a finding is an open subjective-review signal."""
    53→    return (
    54→        finding.get("status") == "open"
    55→        and finding.get("detector") == "subjective_review"
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding: dict, *, finding_id: str = "") -> bool:
    60→    """Best-effort check for holistic subjective-review coverage findings."""
    61→    candidate_id = str(finding.get("id") or finding_id or "")
    62→    if "::holistic_unreviewed" in candidate_id or "::holistic_stale" in candidate_id:
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    summary = str(finding.get("summary", "") or "").lower()
    66→    if "holistic" in summary and "review" in summary:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    70→    return bool(detail.get("holistic"))
    71→
    72→
    73→def subjective_review_open_breakdown(
    74→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    75→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    76→    """Return open subjective count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    77→    reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    holistic_reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    79→    total = 0
    80→
    81→    for finding_id, finding in _iter_findings(findings):
    82→        if not is_subjective_review_open(finding):
    83→            continue
    84→
    85→        total += 1
    86→        reason = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("reason", "other") or "other")
    87→        reason_counts[reason] = reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    88→
    89→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding, finding_id=finding_id):
    90→            holistic_reason_counts[reason] = holistic_reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    91→
    92→    return total, reason_counts, holistic_reason_counts
    93→
    94→
    95→def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    96→    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    97→    if not dim_scores:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    unassessed: list[str] = []
   101→    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
   102→        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
   103→        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
   104→            continue
   105→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   106→        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
   107→            unassessed.append(name)
   108→            continue
   109→        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
   110→        issues = int(info.get("issues", 0))
   111→        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
   112→            unassessed.append(name)
   113→
   114→    unassessed.sort()
   115→    return unassessed
   116→
```

1→"""Scorecard dimension row helpers for the engine/planning layer.
     2→
     3→Provides ``scorecard_dimension_rows`` without importing app-layer modules.
     4→The app scorecard surface re-exports this so external call-sites are unaffected.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS
    10→
    11→
    12→def scorecard_dimension_rows(
    13→    state: dict,
    14→    *,
    15→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    17→    """Return scorecard rows using canonical dimension ordering.
    18→
    19→    Tries ``prepare_scorecard_dimensions`` from the app layer when available
    20→    (deferred import to avoid hard engine -> app dependency), then falls back
    21→    to a simple mechanical-dimension listing.
    22→    """
    23→    if dim_scores is None:
    24→        dim_scores = (
    25→            state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
    26→        )
    27→        projected_state = state
    28→    else:
    29→        projected_state = dict(state)
    30→        projected_state["dimension_scores"] = dim_scores
    31→
    32→    # Try the full app-layer scorecard projection when it can be imported.
    33→    try:
    34→        from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    35→            prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    36→        )
    37→
    38→        rows = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(projected_state)
    39→        if rows:
    40→            return rows
    41→    except ImportError as exc:
    42→        _ = exc
    43→
    44→    # Fallback for synthetic/unit-test states without full scorecard context.
    45→    fallback_dim_scores = dim_scores or {}
    46→    if not isinstance(fallback_dim_scores, dict):
    47→        return []
    48→
    49→    mechanical_names = [dimension.name for dimension in DIMENSIONS]
    50→    fallback_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
    51→    for name in mechanical_names:
    52→        data = fallback_dim_scores.get(name)
    53→        if isinstance(data, dict):
    54→            fallback_rows.append((name, data))
    55→    fallback_rows.extend(
    56→        sorted(
    57→            [
    58→                (name, data)
    59→                for name, data in fallback_dim_scores.items()
    60→                if name not in mechanical_names and isinstance(data, dict)
    61→            ],
    62→            key=lambda item: item[0].lower(),
    63→        )
    64→    )
    65→    return fallback_rows
    66→
```

    90→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})

    97→    return (

    99→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
   100→    )

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass
    16→class ReconcileResult:
    17→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    18→
    19→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    20→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    changes: int = 0
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_candidates(
    26→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    27→) -> list[str]:
    28→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    29→    candidates: list[str] = []
    30→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    31→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    32→            continue
    33→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    34→            candidates.append(fid)
    35→    return candidates
    36→
    37→
    38→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    40→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    41→    if finding is None:
    42→        return False
    43→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _supersede_id(
    47→    plan: PlanModel,
    48→    state: StateModel,
    49→    finding_id: str,
    50→    now: str,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    53→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    54→    detector = ""
    55→    file = ""
    56→    summary = ""
    57→    if finding:
    58→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    60→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    61→
    62→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    63→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    64→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    65→
    66→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    67→        "original_id": finding_id,
    68→        "original_detector": detector,
    69→        "original_file": file,
    70→        "original_summary": summary,
    71→        "status": "superseded",
    72→        "superseded_at": now,
    73→        "remapped_to": None,
    74→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Preserve any existing override note
    78→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    79→    if override and override.get("note"):
    80→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    81→
    82→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    83→
    84→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster finding_ids
    85→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    86→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    87→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    if finding_id in promoted:
    92→        promoted.remove(finding_id)
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    95→        if finding_id in ids:
    96→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    97→
    98→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
    99→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
   100→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   101→        override["cluster"] = None
   102→        override["updated_at"] = now
   103→
   104→    return True
   105→
   106→
   107→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   108→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   109→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   110→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   111→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   112→
   113→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   114→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   115→        try:
   116→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   117→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   118→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   119→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   120→                to_prune.append(fid)
   121→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   122→            to_prune.append(fid)
   123→
   124→    for fid in to_prune:
   125→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   126→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   127→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   128→
   129→    return to_prune
   130→
   131→
   132→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   133→    plan: PlanModel,
   134→    state: StateModel,
   135→) -> ReconcileResult:
   136→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   137→
   138→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   139→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   140→    """
   141→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   142→    result = ReconcileResult()
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   145→
   146→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   147→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   148→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   150→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   151→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   152→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   153→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   154→
   155→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   156→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   157→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   158→    referenced_ids = {
   159→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   160→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   161→        and fid != SYNTHESIS_ID
   162→    }
   163→
   164→    # Check each referenced ID
   165→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   166→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   167→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   168→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   169→                result.changes += 1
   170→
   171→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   172→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   173→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   174→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   175→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   176→            continue
   177→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   178→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   179→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   180→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   181→            result.changes += 1
   182→        if not alive_ids:
   183→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   184→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   185→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   186→        result.changes += 1
   187→
   188→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   189→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   190→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   191→
   192→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   193→    if resurfaced:
   194→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   195→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   196→
   197→    # Prune old superseded entries
   198→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   199→    result.pruned = pruned
   200→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   201→
   202→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   203→    if result.changes > 0:
   204→        from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
   205→
   206→        append_log_entry(
   207→            plan,
   208→            "reconcile",
   209→            finding_ids=result.superseded,
   210→            actor="system",
   211→            detail={
   212→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   213→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   214→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   215→            },
   216→        )
   217→
   218→    return result
   219→
   220→
   221→@dataclass
   222→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   223→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   224→
   225→    new_ids: set[str]
   226→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   227→    synthesis_injected: bool
   228→
   229→
   230→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   231→    plan: PlanModel,
   232→    state: StateModel,
   233→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   234→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   235→
   236→    Appends new finding IDs to queue_order and injects synthesis::pending
   237→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new findings to sync.
   238→    """
   239→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   240→        compute_new_finding_ids,
   241→        sync_synthesis_needed,
   242→    )
   243→
   244→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   245→    new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   246→    if not new_ids:
   247→        return None
   248→
   249→    # Add new finding IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   250→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   251→    existing = set(order)
   252→    added: list[str] = []
   253→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   254→        if fid not in existing:
   255→            order.append(fid)
   256→            added.append(fid)
   257→
   258→    # Inject synthesis::pending if needed
   259→    synthesis_result = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, state)
   260→    synthesis_injected = bool(
   261→        synthesis_result and getattr(synthesis_result, "injected", False)
   262→    )
   263→
   264→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   265→        new_ids=new_ids,
   266→        added_to_queue=added,
   267→        synthesis_injected=synthesis_injected,
   268→    )
   269→
   270→

1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(
    21→    followup: SubjectiveFollowup,
    22→    *,
    23→    leading_newline: bool = False,
    24→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
    25→) -> bool:
    26→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    27→
    28→    When *objective_backlog* > 0, quality nudges are suppressed entirely — the
    29→    queue already surfaces subjective work when it becomes actionable.
    30→    Integrity warnings are always shown (anti-gaming safeguard).
    31→
    32→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    33→    """
    34→    printed = False
    35→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    36→    if followup.low_assessed and objective_backlog <= 0:
    37→        print(
    38→            colorize(
    39→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    40→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    41→                "cyan",
    42→            )
    43→        )
    44→        print(
    45→            colorize(
    46→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    47→                "dim",
    48→            )
    49→        )
    50→        printed = True
    51→
    52→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    53→        if printed:
    54→            print()
    55→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    56→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    57→        printed = True
    58→    return printed
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]
    62→
```

1→"""Shared synthesis guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
     9→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    compute_new_finding_ids,
    13→    is_synthesis_stale,
    14→    load_plan,
    15→    synthesis_phase_banner,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    21→    """Structured result from synthesis staleness detection."""
    22→
    23→    is_stale: bool = False
    24→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    25→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    26→
    27→
    28→def synthesis_guardrail_status(
    29→    *,
    30→    plan: dict | None = None,
    31→    state: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    33→    """Pure detection: is synthesis stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    34→    try:
    35→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    36→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    37→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult()
    38→
    39→    resolved_state = state or {}
    40→
    41→    if not is_synthesis_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    42→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    43→
    44→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    45→    if resolved_state:
    46→        new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    47→
    48→    return SynthesisGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_synthesis_guardrail_info(
    52→    *,
    53→    plan: dict | None = None,
    54→    state: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> bool:
    56→    """Print yellow info banner if synthesis is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    57→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    58→    if not result.is_stale:
    59→        return False
    60→
    61→    if result.new_ids:
    62→        print(colorize(
    63→            f"  {len(result.new_ids)} new review finding(s) not yet triaged.",
    64→            "yellow",
    65→        ))
    66→
    67→    if result._plan is not None:
    68→        banner = synthesis_phase_banner(result._plan)
    69→        if banner:
    70→            print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    71→
    72→    return True
    73→
    74→
    75→def require_synthesis_current_or_exit(
    76→    *,
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    bypass: bool = False,
    79→    attest: str = "",
    80→) -> None:
    81→    """Gate: exit(1) if synthesis is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
    82→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(state=state)
    83→    if not result.is_stale:
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
    87→        print(colorize(
    88→            "  Synthesis guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
    89→            "yellow",
    90→        ))
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    94→    print(colorize(
    95→        f"\n  BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review finding(s) have not been triaged.",
    96→        "red",
    97→    ))
    98→    if new_ids:
    99→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   100→            f = state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {})
   101→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}", "red"))
   102→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   104→    print()
   105→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP: desloppify plan synthesize", "yellow"))
   106→    print(colorize(
   107→        "  (Review new findings, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)",
   108→        "dim",
   109→    ))
   110→    print()
   111→    print(colorize("  View new items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   112→    print(colorize(
   113→        '  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."',
   114→        "dim",
   115→    ))
   116→    print()
   117→    sys.exit(1)
   118→
   119→
   120→__all__ = [
   121→    "SynthesisGuardrailResult",
   122→    "print_synthesis_guardrail_info",
   123→    "require_synthesis_current_or_exit",
   124→    "synthesis_guardrail_status",
   125→]
   126→
```

    25→def scorecard_subjective(
    26→    state: dict,
    27→    dim_scores: dict,
    28→) -> list[dict]:
    29→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    30→    if not dim_scores:
    31→        return []
    32→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    33→        state,
    34→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    39→    findings_scoped: dict,
    40→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    41→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    42→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    46→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    47→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    48→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    49→        for item in grouped_items:
    50→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    51→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    52→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    53→            print(
    54→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    55→            )
    56→
    57→
    58→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    59→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    60→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    61→
    62→
    63→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    64→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    65→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    66→        return "[review]"
    67→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    68→        return "[auto]"
    69→    return ""
    70→
    71→
    72→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    73→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    74→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    75→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    76→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    77→}
    78→
    79→
    80→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
    81→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
    82→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
    83→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
    84→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
    85→    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
    86→        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
    87→    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
    88→        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
    89→    else:
    90→        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    91→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
    92→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    93→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    94→
    95→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
    96→    members = item.get("members", [])
    97→    if members:
    98→        # File distribution
    99→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   100→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   101→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   102→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   103→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   104→        else:
   105→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   106→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   107→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   108→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   109→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   110→
   111→        # Sample IDs
   112→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   113→        for m in members[:3]:
   114→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   115→        if len(members) > 3:
   116→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   117→
   118→    # Action
   119→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   120→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   121→    if primary_command:
   122→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   123→
   124→    # Resolution hints
   125→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   126→                   f'--note "<what>" --confirm', "dim"))
   127→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   128→                   "dim"))
   129→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   130→

   132→def _render_item(
   133→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   134→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   135→) -> None:
   136→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   137→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   138→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    # Synthesis-needed item — dedicated compact renderer
   142→    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   143→        print(colorize("  (Tier 1, blocking)", "bold"))
   144→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   145→        print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   146→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   147→        new_since = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   148→        resolved_since = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   149→        if new_since or resolved_since:
   150→            parts = []
   151→            if new_since:
   152→                parts.append(f"{new_since} new")
   153→            if resolved_since:
   154→                parts.append(f"{resolved_since} resolved")
   155→            print(colorize(f"  Changes since last synthesis: {', '.join(parts)}", "dim"))
   156→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify plan synthesize')}", "cyan"))
   157→        print(colorize("  Complete all 4 stages (observe → reflect → organize → commit) before fixing.", "dim"))
   158→        return
   159→
   160→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   161→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   162→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   163→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   164→
   165→    # Effort/type label
   166→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   167→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   168→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   169→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   170→
   171→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   172→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   173→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   174→        subjective_score = float(
   175→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   176→        )
   177→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   178→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}%")
   179→        print(
   180→            colorize(
   181→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   182→                "cyan",
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→        print(colorize(
   186→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   187→            "dim",
   188→        ))
   189→        if explain:
   190→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   191→                "policy",
   192→                "subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.",
   193→            )
   194→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   195→        return
   196→
   197→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   198→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   199→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   200→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   201→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   202→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   203→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   204→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   205→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   206→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   207→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   208→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   209→
   210→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   211→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   212→
   213→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   214→    if isinstance(detail, str):
   215→        detail = {"suggestion": detail}
   216→    if detail.get("lines"):
   217→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   218→    if detail.get("category"):
   219→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   220→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   221→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   222→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   223→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   224→
   225→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   226→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   227→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   228→        if snippet:
   229→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   230→            print(snippet)
   231→
   232→    if dim_scores:
   233→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   234→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   235→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   236→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   237→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   238→            print(
   239→                colorize(
   240→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   241→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   242→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   243→                    "dim",
   244→                )
   245→            )
   246→
   247→    # Score impact estimate
   248→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   249→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   250→        try:
   251→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   252→            if impact > 0:
   253→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   254→            else:
   255→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   256→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   257→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   258→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   259→                    if issues > 1:
   260→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   261→                        if bulk > 0:
   262→                            print(colorize(
   263→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   264→                                "cyan",
   265→                            ))
   266→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   267→            log(f"  score impact estimate skipped: {exc}")
   268→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   269→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   270→        try:
   271→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   272→            if dim_key:
   273→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   274→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   275→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   276→                        continue
   277→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   278→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   279→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   280→                        if drag > 0.01:
   281→                            print(colorize(
   282→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   283→                                "cyan",
   284→                            ))
   285→                        break
   286→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   287→            log(f"  dimension drag estimate skipped: {exc}")
   288→
   289→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   290→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   291→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   292→        similar_count = sum(
   293→            1
   294→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   295→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   296→        )
   297→        if similar_count > 1:
   298→            print(
   299→                colorize(
   300→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   301→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   302→                    "cyan",
   303→                )
   304→            )
   305→    if explain:
   306→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   307→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   308→        base = (
   309→            f"ranked by confidence={confidence}, "
   310→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   311→        )
   312→
   313→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   314→        if dim_scores and detector:
   315→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   316→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   317→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   318→                base += (
   319→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   320→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   321→                )
   322→
   323→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   324→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   325→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   326→            if dim_key:
   327→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   328→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   329→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   330→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   331→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   332→                        break
   333→
   334→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   335→        if policy:
   336→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   337→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))

   340→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   341→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   342→    print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def show_empty_queue(
   346→    queue: dict,
   347→    strict: float | None,
   348→    *,
   349→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   350→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   351→) -> bool:
   352→    if queue.get("items"):
   353→        return False
   354→    # Show before/after comparison when plan_start_strict is available
   355→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   356→        delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
   357→        delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
   358→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   359→        print(colorize(
   360→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   361→            "cyan",
   362→        ))
   363→        print(colorize(
   364→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   365→            "dim",
   366→        ))
   367→    else:
   368→        suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   369→        print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   370→    return True
   371→
   372→
   373→def _render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   374→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   375→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   376→    tag = _effort_tag(item)
   377→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   378→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   379→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   380→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   381→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   382→
   383→
   384→def render_terminal_items(
   385→    items: list[dict],
   386→    dim_scores: dict,
   387→    findings_scoped: dict,
   388→    *,
   389→    group: str,
   390→    explain: bool,
   391→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   392→    plan: dict | None = None,
   393→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   394→) -> None:
   395→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   396→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):

    29→def path_scoped_findings(
    30→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    33→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    34→    return {
    35→        finding_id: finding
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    37→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    42→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    43→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    44→        return True
    45→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    46→    return (
    47→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    48→        or file_path == scan_path
    49→        or file_path == "."
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→def open_scope_breakdown(
    54→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    55→    scan_path: str | None,
    56→    *,
    57→    detector: str | None = None,
    58→) -> dict[str, int]:
    59→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    60→    in_scope = 0
    61→    out_of_scope = 0
    62→
    63→    for finding in findings.values():
    64→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    65→            continue
    66→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    67→            continue
    68→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    69→            continue
    70→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    71→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    72→            in_scope += 1
    73→        else:
    74→            out_of_scope += 1
    75→
    76→    return {
    77→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    78→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    79→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    80→    }
    81→
