    83→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    84→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    85→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    86→
    87→
    88→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    89→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    90→) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    92→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    93→        if "*" in pattern:
    94→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    95→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    96→                return pattern
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        if "::" in pattern:
   100→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   101→                return pattern
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   105→            return pattern
   106→
   107→    return None
   108→
   109→
   110→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   111→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   112→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   113→    matched_ids = [
   114→        finding_id
   115→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   116→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   117→    ]
   118→    now = utc_now()
   119→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   120→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   121→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   122→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   123→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   124→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   125→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   126→
   127→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   128→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   129→    return len(matched_ids)
   130→
   131→
   132→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   133→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   134→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   135→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   136→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   137→    if pattern not in ignores:
   138→        ignores.append(pattern)
   139→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   140→
   141→
   142→def make_finding(
   143→    detector: str,
   144→    file: str,
   145→    name: str,
   146→    *,
   147→    tier: int,
   148→    confidence: str,
   149→    summary: str,
   150→    detail: dict | None = None,
   151→) -> Finding:
   152→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   153→    rfile = rel(file)
   154→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    return {
   157→        "id": finding_id,
   158→        "detector": detector,
   159→        "file": rfile,
   160→        "tier": tier,
   161→        "confidence": confidence,
   162→        "summary": summary,
   163→        "detail": detail or {},
   164→        "status": "open",
   165→        "note": None,
   166→        "first_seen": now,
   167→        "last_seen": now,
   168→        "resolved_at": None,
   169→        "reopen_count": 0,
   170→    }
   171→
   172→
   173→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   178→    if finding_id == pattern:
   179→        return True
   180→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   181→        return True
   182→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   183→        return True
   184→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   185→        return True
   186→    if (
   187→        finding.get("detector") == pattern
   188→        or finding["file"] == pattern
   189→        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   190→    ):
   191→        return True
   192→
   193→    # Name-segment fallback: bare name matches the last ::segment of the ID
   194→    if "::" not in pattern and "::" in finding_id:
   195→        segments = finding_id.split("::")
   196→        name_segment = segments[-1]
   197→        if name_segment == pattern:
   198→            return True
   199→        # For hashed IDs, also match the descriptive name (second-to-last segment)
   200→        if len(segments) >= 3 and _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment) and segments[-2] == pattern:
   201→            return True
   202→
   203→    return False
   204→

## Subjective Dimension Queue Flow: Complete Analysis

### 1. Subjective Dimension Definitions

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/subjective_dimensions.py`**

Subjective dimensions are defined via a metadata system. The canonical list lives in `DISPLAY_NAMES` (lines 20-44), mapping internal keys like `"cross_module_architecture"` to display labels like `"Cross-module arch"`. There are roughly 20 dimensions covering holistic concerns (architecture, elegance, test strategy, etc.) and per-file concerns (naming quality, logic clarity, type safety, contracts).

Each dimension has metadata including:
- `display_name` (human-readable label)
- `weight` (for score aggregation, from `_LEGACY_SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHTS_BY_DISPLAY`)
- `enabled_by_default` (whether it's active without explicit opt-in)
- `reset_on_scan` (whether scores get reset when a new scan runs)

The metadata is loaded from prompt files via `_load_dimensions_payload()` and `_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang()`, with legacy hardcoded fallbacks. The `load_subjective_dimension_metadata()` function merges shared + per-language metadata with LRU caching.

### 2. How Subjective Dimensions Are Tracked in State

Subjective dimension scores live in two places in state:
- **`state["dimension_scores"]`** — the full scorecard, with entries like `{"score": 75.0, "strict": 72.0, "issues": 3, "checks": 10, "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {...}}}`
- **`state["subjective_assessments"]`** — raw assessment records per dimension key, where `payload.get("placeholder")` marks dimensions that have never been reviewed

An "unassessed" dimension is one where either:
- The `subjective_assessment` detector metadata has `"placeholder": True`, OR
- The strict score is 0.0 and there are 0 issues (line 109-111 of `integrity.py`)

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/integrity.py`**, function `unassessed_subjective_dimensions()` (lines 95-115):
```python
def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
            continue
        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
            unassessed.append(name)
            continue
        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
        issues = int(info.get("issues", 0))
        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
            unassessed.append(name)
```

### 3. How Subjective Dimensions Enter the Queue

There are **two parallel paths** for getting subjective items into the work queue:

#### Path A: Plan Queue Sync (stale_dimensions.py)

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`**

During scan workflow (`scan_workflow.py`, lines 196-206), three sync functions run in sequence:

1. **`sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)`** (lines 124-162) — This is the "initial review" path:
   - Looks up `state["subjective_assessments"]` for entries with `"placeholder": True`
   - Generates IDs like `subjective::design_coherence`
   - **Prunes** any `subjective::*` IDs from `queue_order` that are no longer unscored AND not stale
   - **Prepends** unscored IDs to the **front** of `queue_order` (after any promoted items)
   - This ensures unscored dimensions appear first in plan ordering

2. **`sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)`** (lines 169-208) — This is the "re-review" path:
   - Looks up `scorecard_subjective_entries()` for entries with `"stale": True` (the `needs_review_refresh` flag)
   - **Only injects stale IDs when no objective items remain** in the queue (line 200: `has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))`)
   - Appends to the **back** of the queue

3. **`sync_synthesis_needed(plan, state)`** — Handles synthesis injection (separate concern)

#### Path B: Queue Build Time (helpers.py)

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`**

The function `build_subjective_items()` (lines 239-352) creates synthetic queue items at build time:

1. Calls `scorecard_subjective_entries()` to get all subjective dimension data
2. Calls `unassessed_subjective_dimensions()` to identify placeholders
3. For each subjective entry below the threshold score:
   - Determines if it's `is_unassessed` (placeholder, or in unassessed_dims, or score=0 with 0 issues)
   - Determines if it's `is_stale` (needs_review_refresh flag)
   - Sets `is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed` — meaning **unassessed items are NEVER backlog-gated**, but re-review items with no existing review findings ARE gated
   - Creates items with `kind: "subjective_dimension"` and IDs like `subjective::design_coherence`

### 4. The scorecard_subjective_entries() Function

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py`** (lines 70-122)

This is the canonical source for which dimensions are "subjective" and their current scores:

```python
def scorecard_subjective_entries(state, *, dim_scores=None):
    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    # ...
    for name, data in rows:
        is_subjective = ("subjective_assessment" in detectors
                         or lowered_name in subjective_display_names)
        if not is_subjective:
            continue
        placeholder = bool(
            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
            or (data.get("score", 0) == 0
                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0)
        )
```

**Critical detail**: The `placeholder` flag is set when either the detector metadata says so, or when score/issues/checks are all zero. The `stale` flag is set when `assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")` is truthy.

### 5. Queue Sorting and Gating

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`**

The `item_sort_key()` function (lines 83-119) determines the mechanical sort order:

```python
def item_sort_key(item):
    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
        return (-1, 0, 0, ...)  # Always first

    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
        return (0, action_priority, -member_count, ...)  # Second

    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
        return (1, 1, subjective_score_value, ...)  # After mechanical findings

    # Regular findings:
        return (1, 0, confidence_order, ...)  # Before subjective at same level
```

**Key insight**: In the mechanical sort, subjective items sort **AFTER** regular findings. The tuple `(1, 1, ...)` sorts after `(1, 0, ...)`. This means by default, all mechanical/objective findings appear before any subjective work.

### 6. Queue Construction in build_work_queue()

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`** (lines 299-402)

The full queue build process:

1. **Build finding items** (mechanical findings from state)
2. **Build subjective items** (synthetic items from `build_subjective_items()`)
3. **Backlog gating** (lines 343-345): Items with `backlog_gated=True` are suppressed when `has_objective_backlog` is true. **But unassessed items are never backlog_gated** (see helpers.py line 307: `is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed`).
4. **Sort by `item_sort_key`** — puts subjective items after mechanical items
5. **Apply plan order** via `_apply_plan_order()` — this reorders items based on `plan["queue_order"]`, which has unscored IDs prepended to the front by `sync_unscored_dimensions()`
6. **Collapse clusters** — may merge subjective items into `auto/initial-review` cluster
7. **Interleave** via `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail()` — alternates 3 objective, 1 subjective to prevent starvation

### 7. Auto-clustering of Subjective Items

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`** (lines 361-458)

The `auto_cluster_findings()` function creates two special clusters:

- **`auto/initial-review`** (cluster_key: `subjective::unscored`) — groups ALL unscored subjective dimensions. Minimum size is 1 (not 2 like other clusters). These are the "never reviewed" dimensions.
- **`auto/stale-review`** (cluster_key: `subjective::stale`) — groups stale (previously scored but outdated) dimensions. Minimum size is 2.

### 8. Where the "Initial Review Blocking" Behavior Should Happen (and the Gap)

The design intent is clear from the code:

**Intent**: Unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions should appear at the TOP of the work queue.

**How it's implemented**:
1. `sync_unscored_dimensions()` **prepends** unscored IDs to `plan["queue_order"]` — this puts them first in the plan ordering
2. `_apply_plan_order()` in `core.py` reorders items so that anything in `queue_order` appears first, in order
3. Unassessed items are **never backlog_gated** — they always pass through
4. The `auto/initial-review` cluster groups them for a single action

**However, there is NO explicit "hide all other items" behavior.** Here is the gap:

- The interleave guardrail (`_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail`) actually works in the **opposite direction** — it intersperses objective items between subjective items (3 objective, then 1 subjective). This would dilute the "initial review first" intent.
- When `collapse_clusters` is active and there's an `auto/initial-review` cluster, the cluster item goes through `item_sort_key()` which gives clusters sort key `(0, ...)` — meaning clusters sort **before** individual findings `(1, ...)`. So the `auto/initial-review` cluster would appear first.
- But if clusters are NOT collapsed (e.g., when drilling into a specific cluster, or when the plan order is applied), individual `subjective_dimension` items sort with key `(1, 1, ...)` which is AFTER mechanical findings `(1, 0, ...)`.
- The plan order (`_apply_plan_order()`) overrides the mechanical sort for items in `queue_order`, so unscored items prepended to the front DO appear first. But the interleave guardrail runs AFTER plan ordering and can shuffle them.

**Summary of the current behavior**:

| Scenario | Unscored items position | Other items hidden? |
|---|---|---|
| With plan + cluster collapse | `auto/initial-review` cluster sorts first (key `(0, ...)`) | No, other items visible below |
| With plan, no cluster collapse | Plan order puts them first, but interleave may shuffle | No |
| Without plan | Mechanical sort puts them AFTER objective findings | No |
| `count=1` (default `next`) | First item from the sorted/ordered list | Yes, by virtue of `count=1` |

**The "hide all other items until completed" behavior does NOT currently exist.** The closest mechanism is:
1. Plan ordering prepends unscored to front
2. `count=1` in the default `next` command means only the top item shows
3. The `auto/initial-review` cluster collapses them into a single meta-item that sorts before individual findings

But if you ask for `count=10` or use `status` or `plan queue`, all items are visible. There is no queue-level gating that suppresses non-subjective items when unassessed dimensions exist. The `backlog_gated` mechanism works in the **reverse direction** — it suppresses stale subjective re-review items while objective backlog exists, not the other way around.

    16→def _lang_from_scan_history(state: dict) -> str | None:
    17→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    18→    if not isinstance(history, list):

    29→def _lang_from_capabilities(state: dict) -> str | None:

    31→    if not isinstance(capabilities, dict) or len(capabilities) != 1:

> AGENT

I found it. The function `prepare_scorecard_dimensions()` **does NOT intentionally filter out subjective dimensions**. Here's what actually happens:

**The filtering logic (lines 218-222):**

```python
active_dims = [
    (name, data)
    for name, data in all_dims
    if data.get("checks", 0) > 0 and not is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data)
]
```

This **excludes dimensions if:**
1. `checks` count is 0 or missing (line 221: `data.get("checks", 0) > 0`)
2. They are unassessed subjective placeholders (line 221: `not is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data)`)

**The placeholder check (lines 66-71):**
```python
def is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data: dict) -> bool:
    return (
        "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
        and data.get("score", 0) == 0
        and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    )
```

**The reason you're only seeing 5 mechanical rows:** Subjective dimensions like "Naming quality" and "Logic clarity" are likely being filtered out because:
- They don't have `checks > 0`, OR
- They match the placeholder condition (score=0, issues=0, has subjective_assessment detector)

Then, `limit_scorecard_dimensions()` (lines 146-195) applies a hard cap (default 5 rows from `SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS`) that **prioritizes mechanical dimensions first** (lines 156-167), only including subjective dimensions if there's room in the budget.

**Bottom line:** It's intentional filtering, but it's **data-driven, not name-driven**. Subjective dimensions are included IF they have real data (`checks > 0`) and pass the placeholder check, then they compete for the remaining slots after mechanical dimensions are included.

    43→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    44→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    45→
    46→    items: list[dict]

    52→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    53→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    54→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    *,
    60→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    61→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    62→) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    64→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    65→        return items
    66→
    67→    subjective_items = deque(
    68→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    69→    )
    70→    objective_items = deque(
    71→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    72→    )
    73→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    74→        return items
    75→
    76→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    77→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    78→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    79→            if not objective_items:
    80→                break
    81→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    82→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    83→            if not subjective_items:
    84→                break
    85→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    86→
    87→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    88→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    89→    return interleaved
    90→
    91→
    92→def _new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    93→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    94→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    95→    if not scan_history:
    96→        return set()
    97→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    98→    if not threshold:
    99→        return set()
   100→    return {
   101→        fid
   102→        for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   103→        if f.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
   104→    }
   105→
   106→
   107→def _apply_plan_order(
   108→    items: list[dict],
   109→    plan: dict,
   110→    *,
   111→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   112→    cluster: str | None = None,
   113→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   114→) -> list[dict]:
   115→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   116→
   117→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   118→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   119→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   120→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   121→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   122→    """
   123→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   124→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   125→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   126→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   127→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   128→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   129→
   130→    # Build lookup
   131→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   132→    for item in items:
   133→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   134→
   135→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   136→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   137→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   138→        if override.get("description"):
   139→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   140→        if override.get("note"):
   141→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   142→        if override.get("cluster"):
   143→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   144→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   145→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   146→                "name": cluster_name,
   147→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   148→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   149→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   150→            }
   151→
   152→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   153→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   154→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   155→    for fid in queue_order:
   156→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   157→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   158→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   159→
   160→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   161→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   162→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   163→    _new = new_ids or set()
   164→    for item in items:
   165→        item_id = item["id"]
   166→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   167→            continue
   168→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   169→            skipped_items.append(item)
   170→        elif item_id in _new:
   171→            remaining_new.append(item)
   172→        else:
   173→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   174→
   175→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   176→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   177→    if include_skipped:
   178→        result = result + skipped_items
   179→
   180→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   181→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   182→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   183→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   184→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   185→            if skip_entry:
   186→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   187→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   188→                if skip_reason:
   189→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   190→
   191→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   192→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   193→    if effective_cluster:
   194→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   195→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   196→        if cluster_member_ids:
   197→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   198→
   199→    return result
   200→
   201→
   202→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   203→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   204→    try:
   205→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   206→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   207→        if meta:
   208→            return meta.action_type
   209→    except ImportError as exc:
   210→        _ = exc
   211→    return "manual_fix"
   212→
   213→
   214→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   215→
   216→
   217→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   218→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   219→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   220→    if not clusters:
   221→        return items
   222→
   223→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   224→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   225→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   226→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   227→            continue
   228→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   229→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   230→
   231→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   232→        return items
   233→
   234→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   235→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   236→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   237→
   238→    for item in items:
   239→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   240→        if cname:
   241→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   242→        else:
   243→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   244→
   245→    # Build cluster meta-items
   246→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   247→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   248→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   249→        if len(members) < 2:
   250→            result.extend(members)
   251→            continue
   252→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   253→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   254→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   255→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   256→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   257→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   258→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   259→            action_type = "reorganize"
   260→        else:
   261→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   262→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   263→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   264→                action_type = "refactor"
   265→
   266→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   267→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   268→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   269→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   270→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   271→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   272→        else:
   273→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   274→
   275→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   276→        if not primary_command:
   277→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   278→
   279→        meta_item: dict = {
   280→            "id": cname,
   281→            "kind": "cluster",
   282→            "action_type": action_type,
   283→            "summary": summary,
   284→            "members": members,
   285→            "member_count": len(members),
   286→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   287→            "cluster_name": cname,
   288→            "cluster_auto": True,
   289→            "confidence": "high",
   290→            "detector": detector,
   291→            "file": "",
   292→        }
   293→        result.append(meta_item)
   294→
   295→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   296→    return result
   297→
   298→
   299→def build_work_queue(
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   303→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   304→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   305→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   306→
   307→    status = resolved_options.status
   308→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   309→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   310→    try:
   311→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   312→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   313→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   314→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   315→
   316→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   317→        state,
   318→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   319→        status_filter=status,
   320→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   321→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   322→    )
   323→
   324→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   325→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   326→    suppressed_count = 0
   327→    if (
   328→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   329→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   330→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   331→    ):
   332→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   333→            state,
   334→            state.get("findings", {}),
   335→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   336→        )
   337→        for item in subjective_items:
   338→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   339→                continue
   340→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   341→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   342→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   343→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   344→                suppressed_count += 1
   345→                continue
   346→            all_items.append(item)
   347→
   348→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   349→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   350→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   351→        if synth_item is not None:
   352→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   353→
   354→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   355→
   356→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   357→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   358→    if resolved_options.plan:
   359→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   360→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   361→            all_items,
   362→            resolved_options.plan,
   363→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   364→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   365→            new_ids=new_ids,
   366→        )
   367→
   368→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   369→    should_collapse = (
   370→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   371→        and resolved_options.plan
   372→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   373→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   374→    )
   375→    if should_collapse:
   376→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   377→
   378→    should_interleave = (
   379→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   380→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   381→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   382→        and not resolved_options.scope
   383→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   384→    )
   385→    if should_interleave:
   386→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   387→
   388→    total = len(all_items)
   389→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   390→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   391→
   392→    if resolved_options.explain:
   393→        for item in all_items:
   394→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   395→
   396→    return {
   397→        "items": all_items,
   398→        "total": total,
   399→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   400→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   401→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   402→    }
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = [
   406→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   407→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   408→    "WorkQueueResult",
   409→    "build_work_queue",
   410→    "group_queue_items",
   411→]
   412→

   216→    items = queue.get("items", [])

   237→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display

    50→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    51→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    52→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    53→            print(
    54→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    55→            )
    56→
    57→
    58→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    59→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    60→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    61→
    62→
    63→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    64→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    65→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    66→        return "[review]"
    67→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    68→        return "[auto]"
    69→    return ""
    70→
    71→
    72→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    73→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    74→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    75→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    76→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    77→}
    78→
    79→
    80→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
    81→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
    82→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
    83→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
    84→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
    85→    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
    86→        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
    87→    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
    88→        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
    89→    else:
    90→        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    91→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
    92→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    93→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    94→
    95→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
    96→    members = item.get("members", [])
    97→    if members:
    98→        # File distribution
    99→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   100→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   101→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   102→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   103→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   104→        else:
   105→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   106→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   107→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   108→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   109→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   110→
   111→        # Sample IDs
   112→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   113→        for m in members[:3]:
   114→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   115→        if len(members) > 3:
   116→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   117→
   118→    # Action
   119→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   120→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   121→    if primary_command:
   122→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   123→
   124→    # Resolution hints
   125→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   126→                   f'--note "<what>" --confirm', "dim"))
   127→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   128→                   "dim"))
   129→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   130→
   131→
   132→def _render_item(
   133→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   134→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   135→) -> None:
   136→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   137→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   138→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    # Synthesis-needed item — dedicated compact renderer
   142→    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   143→        print(colorize("  (Tier 1, blocking)", "bold"))
   144→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   145→        print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   146→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   147→        new_since = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   148→        resolved_since = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   149→        if new_since or resolved_since:
   150→            parts = []
   151→            if new_since:
   152→                parts.append(f"{new_since} new")
   153→            if resolved_since:
   154→                parts.append(f"{resolved_since} resolved")
   155→            print(colorize(f"  Changes since last synthesis: {', '.join(parts)}", "dim"))
   156→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify plan synthesize')}", "cyan"))
   157→        print(colorize("  Complete all 4 stages (observe → reflect → organize → commit) before fixing.", "dim"))
   158→        return
   159→
   160→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   161→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   162→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   163→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   164→
   165→    # Effort/type label
   166→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   167→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   168→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   169→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   170→
   171→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   172→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   173→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   174→        subjective_score = float(
   175→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   176→        )
   177→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   178→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}%")
   179→        print(
   180→            colorize(
   181→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   182→                "cyan",
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→        print(colorize(
   186→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   187→            "dim",
   188→        ))
   189→        if explain:
   190→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   191→                "policy",
   192→                "subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.",
   193→            )
   194→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   195→        return
   196→
   197→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   198→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   199→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   200→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   201→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   202→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   203→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   204→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   205→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   206→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   207→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   208→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   209→
   210→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   211→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   212→
   213→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   214→    if isinstance(detail, str):
   215→        detail = {"suggestion": detail}
   216→    if detail.get("lines"):
   217→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   218→    if detail.get("category"):
   219→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   220→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   221→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   222→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   223→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   224→
   225→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   226→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   227→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   228→        if snippet:
   229→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   230→            print(snippet)
   231→
   232→    if dim_scores:
   233→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   234→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   235→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   236→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   237→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   238→            print(
   239→                colorize(
   240→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   241→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   242→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   243→                    "dim",
   244→                )
   245→            )
   246→
   247→    # Score impact estimate
   248→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   249→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   250→        try:
   251→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   252→            if impact > 0:
   253→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   254→            else:
   255→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   256→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   257→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   258→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   259→                    if issues > 1:
   260→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   261→                        if bulk > 0:
   262→                            print(colorize(
   263→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   264→                                "cyan",
   265→                            ))
   266→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   267→            log(f"  score impact estimate skipped: {exc}")
   268→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   269→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   270→        try:
   271→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   272→            if dim_key:
   273→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   274→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   275→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   276→                        continue
   277→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   278→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   279→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   280→                        if drag > 0.01:
   281→                            print(colorize(
   282→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   283→                                "cyan",
   284→                            ))
   285→                        break
   286→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   287→            log(f"  dimension drag estimate skipped: {exc}")
   288→
   289→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   290→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   291→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   292→        similar_count = sum(
   293→            1
   294→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   295→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   296→        )
   297→        if similar_count > 1:
   298→            print(
   299→                colorize(
   300→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   301→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   302→                    "cyan",
   303→                )
   304→            )
   305→    if explain:
   306→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   307→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   308→        base = (
   309→            f"ranked by confidence={confidence}, "
   310→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   311→        )
   312→
   313→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   314→        if dim_scores and detector:
   315→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   316→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   317→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   318→                base += (
   319→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   320→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   321→                )
   322→
   323→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   324→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   325→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   326→            if dim_key:
   327→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   328→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   329→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   330→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   331→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   332→                        break
   333→
   334→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   335→        if policy:
   336→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   337→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   338→
   339→
   340→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   341→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   342→    print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def show_empty_queue(
   346→    queue: dict,
   347→    strict: float | None,
   348→    *,
   349→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   350→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   351→) -> bool:
   352→    if queue.get("items"):
   353→        return False
   354→    # Show before/after comparison when plan_start_strict is available
   355→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   356→        delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
   357→        delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
   358→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   359→        print(colorize(
   360→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   361→            "cyan",
   362→        ))
   363→        print(colorize(
   364→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   365→            "dim",
   366→        ))
   367→    else:
   368→        suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   369→        print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   370→    return True
   371→
   372→
   373→def _render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   374→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   375→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   376→    tag = _effort_tag(item)
   377→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   378→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   379→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   380→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   381→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   382→
   383→
   384→def render_terminal_items(
   385→    items: list[dict],
   386→    dim_scores: dict,
   387→    findings_scoped: dict,
   388→    *,
   389→    group: str,
   390→    explain: bool,
   391→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   392→    plan: dict | None = None,
   393→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   394→) -> None:
   395→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   396→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   397→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   398→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   399→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   400→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   401→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   402→
   403→    if group != "item":
   404→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   405→        return
   406→
   407→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   408→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   409→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   410→    )
   411→
   412→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   413→        if idx > 0:
   414→            print()
   415→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   416→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   417→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   418→            continue
   419→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   420→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   421→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   422→        elif len(items) > 1:
   423→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   424→        else:
   425→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   426→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   427→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   428→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   429→
   430→
   431→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   432→    if len(items) != 1:
   433→        return
   434→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   435→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   436→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   437→        return
   438→    item = items[0]
   439→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   440→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   441→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   442→        primary = item.get(
   443→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   444→        )
   445→        print(f"    {primary}")
   446→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   447→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   448→        return
   449→
   450→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   451→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   452→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   453→        print(f"    {primary}")
   454→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   455→    else:
   456→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   457→
   458→    print(
   459→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   460→    )
   461→    print(
   462→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   463→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   464→    )
   465→
   466→
   467→def render_followup_nudges(
   468→    state: dict,
   469→    dim_scores: dict,
   470→    findings_scoped: dict,
   471→    *,
   472→    strict_score: float | None,
   473→    target_strict_score: float,
   474→    queue_total: int = 0,
   475→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   476→    breakdown: "QueueBreakdown | None" = None,
   477→) -> None:
   478→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   479→        QueueBreakdown,
   480→        format_queue_block,
   481→    )
   482→
   483→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   484→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   485→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   486→        state,
   487→        subjective_entries,
   488→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   489→        max_quality_items=3,
   490→        max_integrity_items=5,
   491→    )
   492→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   493→    # Show frozen plan-start score + queue block when in an active cycle
   494→    if queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None and breakdown is not None:
   495→        frozen = plan_start_strict
   496→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen)
   497→        print()
   498→        for text, style in block:
   499→            print(colorize(text, style))
   500→        print(colorize(
   501→            "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   502→            "dim",
   503→        ))
   504→    elif queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None:
   505→        print(
   506→            colorize(
   507→                f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100",
   508→                "cyan",
   509→            )
   510→        )
   511→        print(
   512→            colorize(
   513→                f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   514→                " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   515→                "dim",
   516→            )
   517→        )
   518→    elif strict_score is not None:
   519→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   520→        if gap > 0:
   521→            print(
   522→                colorize(
   523→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   524→                    "cyan",
   525→                )
   526→            )
   527→        else:
   528→            print(
   529→                colorize(
   530→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   531→                    "green",
   532→                )
   533→            )
   534→    # Show queue block after north star when no frozen score
   535→    if breakdown is not None and queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is None:
   536→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   537→        for text, style in block:
   538→            print(colorize(text, style))
   539→
   540→    # Subjective bottleneck banner — only shown when the objective queue is
   541→    # clear.  While objective items remain, the queue is the single authority
   542→    # on what to work on next; no need to distract with subjective advice.
   543→    _objective_remaining = max(
   544→        0,
   545→        (breakdown.queue_total - breakdown.subjective) if breakdown else queue_total,
   546→    )
   547→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores and _objective_remaining <= 0:
   548→        try:
   549→            health_breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   550→            subjective_drag = sum(
   551→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   552→                for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   553→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   554→            )
   555→            mechanical_drag = sum(
   556→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   557→                for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   558→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   559→            )
   560→            if subjective_drag > mechanical_drag and subjective_drag > 5.0:
   561→                print(colorize(
   562→                    f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   563→                    f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   564→                    "yellow",
   565→                ))
   566→                print(colorize(
   567→                    "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   568→                    "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   569→                    "to re-score.",
   570→                    "yellow",
   571→                ))
   572→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   573→            log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   574→
   575→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   576→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   577→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   578→
   579→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   580→    if queue_total > 10:
   581→        print(colorize(
   582→            "\n  Tip: after structural fixes (splitting files, moving code), rescan to "
   583→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   584→            "dim",
   585→        ))
   586→
   587→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   588→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   589→        findings_scoped
   590→    )
   591→    parts: list[str] = []
   592→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   593→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   594→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   595→    open_review = [
   596→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   597→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   598→    ]
   599→    if low_dims:
   600→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   601→    if stale_count:
   602→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   603→    if unassessed_count:
   604→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   605→    if len(open_review):
   606→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   607→    if coverage_open > 0:
   608→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   609→
   610→    if parts:
   611→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   612→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   613→
   614→
   615→__all__ = [
   616→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   617→    "render_followup_nudges",
   618→    "render_queue_header",
   619→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   620→    "render_terminal_items",
   621→    "scorecard_subjective",
   622→    "show_empty_queue",
   623→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   624→]

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    35→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    36→        "file": item.get("file"),
    37→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    38→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    39→        "status": item.get("status"),
    40→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    41→    }
    42→    explain = item.get("explain")
    43→    if explain is not None:
    44→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    45→
    46→    # Plan metadata
    47→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    48→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    49→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    50→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    52→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    54→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    56→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    57→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    58→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    59→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    60→
    61→    return serialized
    62→
    63→
    64→def build_query_payload(
    65→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    66→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    67→    *,
    68→    command: str,
    69→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    70→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    71→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    72→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    73→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    74→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
    75→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    76→        "suppressed_count": queue.get("suppressed_count", 0),
    77→    }
    78→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    79→        "command": command,
    80→        "items": serialized,
    81→        "queue": queue_section,
    82→        "narrative": narrative,
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    if plan and (
    86→        plan.get("queue_order")
    87→        or plan.get("skipped")
    88→        or plan.get("clusters")
    89→    ):
    90→        clusters_summary = []
    91→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    92→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    93→            clusters_summary.append({
    94→                "name": name,
    95→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
    96→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
    97→            })
    98→        payload["plan"] = {
    99→            "active": True,
   100→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   101→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   102→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   103→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   104→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   105→        }
   106→
   107→    return payload
   108→
   109→
   110→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   111→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   112→    lines = [
   113→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   114→        "",
   115→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   116→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   117→    ]
   118→    for item in items:
   119→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   120→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   121→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   122→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   123→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   124→    lines.append("")
   125→    return "\n".join(lines)
   126→
   127→
   128→def write_output_file(
   129→    output_file: str,
   130→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   131→    item_count: int,
   132→    *,
   133→    safe_write_text_fn,
   134→    colorize_fn,
   135→) -> bool:
   136→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   137→    try:
   138→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   139→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   140→    except OSError as exc:
   141→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   142→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   143→        return False
   144→    return True
   145→
   146→
   147→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   148→    output_format: str,
   149→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   150→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   151→) -> bool:
   152→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   153→    renderers = {
   154→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   155→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   156→    }
   157→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   158→    if renderer is None:
   159→        return False
   160→    renderer()
   161→    return True
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "build_query_payload",
   166→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   167→    "render_markdown",
   168→    "serialize_item",
   169→    "write_output_file",
   170→]
   171→

    52→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    77→        )
    78→        return

    80→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    81→        return
    82→
    83→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    84→    if stale_warning:

    86→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    87→    if skill_warning:
    88→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    89→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    90→    if config_warning:
    91→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    92→
    93→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    94→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    95→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    96→
    97→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    98→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    99→
   100→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering and narrative
   101→    _plan = load_plan()
   102→    _plan_active = _plan if (
   103→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ) else None
   105→
   106→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=_plan, state=state)

   111→    )
   112→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
   113→
   114→    # Show frozen plan-start score when in an active queue cycle
   115→    _plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(_plan)
   116→    _breakdown = None
   117→    _status_queue_remaining = 0
   118→    if _plan_start_strict is not None:

   121→            _status_queue_remaining = _breakdown.queue_total
   122→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:

   124→            _status_queue_remaining = 0
   125→    if _plan_start_strict is not None and _status_queue_remaining > 0:
   126→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   127→            _plan, _status_queue_remaining, breakdown=_breakdown,
   128→        )

   142→            except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:

   144→        if _breakdown is not None and _breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   145→            block = format_queue_block(_breakdown)
   146→            for text, style in block:

   148→    print_scan_metrics(state)
   149→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)
   150→    print_scan_completeness(state)
   151→
   152→    # Compute objective backlog once for consistent subjective actionability gating
   153→    _objective_backlog = 0
   154→    if _breakdown is not None:
   155→        _objective_backlog = max(0, _breakdown.queue_total - _breakdown.subjective)
   156→
   157→    if dim_scores:

   159→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(

   189→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))

   192→    write_status_query(

   205→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   206→        plan=_plan_active,

   210→def _status_json_payload(
   211→    state: dict,
   212→    stats: dict,
   213→    dim_scores: dict,
   214→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   215→    subjective_measures: list[dict],

   218→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   219→    findings = state.get("findings", {})

   222→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   223→        else None
   224→    )
   225→    return {
   226→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   227→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   228→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   229→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   230→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   231→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,

   233→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   234→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   235→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   236→        "stats": stats,
   237→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   238→        "suppression": suppression,
   239→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   240→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   241→    }

     2→

     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query

    22→    scorecard_subjective_entries,

    26→from desloppify.scoring import (
    27→    DIMENSIONS,
    28→    compute_health_breakdown,
    29→    compute_score_impact,
    30→    merge_potentials,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    33→
    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",
    99→            "overall_score": overall_score,
   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   101→            "strict_score": strict_score,
   102→            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_score,
   103→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   104→            "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   105→            "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   106→            "stats": stats,
   107→            "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,
   110→            "ignores": ignores,
   111→            "suppression": suppression,
   112→            "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   113→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   114→            "open_scope": open_scope,
   115→            "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),
   117→            "narrative": narrative,
   118→            **({"plan": {
   119→                "active": True,
   120→                "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   121→                "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   122→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   124→            }} if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped")) else {}),
   125→        }
   126→    )
   127→
   128→
   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   132→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   133→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))
   134→
   135→    last_ignored = int(suppression.get("last_ignored", 0) or 0)
   136→    last_raw = int(suppression.get("last_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   137→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   138→
   139→    if last_raw > 0:
   140→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   144→                style,
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   148→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   149→
   150→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"    Recent ({recent_scans} scans): {recent_ignored}/{recent_raw} findings hidden ({recent_pct:.1f}%)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )

   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   168→    )
