```
   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _render_dimension_legend(
   291→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   292→    state: dict | None = None,
   293→    *,
   294→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
   295→) -> None:
   296→    """Print the legend footer and, when actionable, the stale rerun command."""
   297→    print(
   298→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   299→    )
   300→    print(
   301→        colorize(
   302→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   303→            "dim",
   304→        )
   305→    )
   306→    stale_keys = [
   307→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   308→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   309→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   310→    ]
   311→    if stale_keys:
   312→        print(
   313→            colorize("  [stale] = assessment outdated", "yellow")
   314→        )
   315→        if objective_backlog <= 0:
   316→            n = len(stale_keys)
   317→            dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   318→            print(
   319→                colorize(
   320→                    f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   321→                    f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg} --force-review-rerun`",
   322→                    "yellow",
   323→                )
   324→            )
   325→
   326→
   327→def show_dimension_table(
   328→    state: dict, dim_scores: dict, *, objective_backlog: int = 0,
   329→) -> None:
   330→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   331→    print()
   332→    bar_len = 20
   333→    print(
   334→        colorize(
   335→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   336→            "dim",
   337→        )
   338→    )
   339→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   340→
   341→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   342→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   343→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   344→
   345→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   346→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   347→        scorecard_subjective,
   348→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   349→        bar_len=bar_len,
   350→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   351→    )
   352→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state, objective_backlog=objective_backlog)
   353→    print()
   354→
   355→
   356→def show_focus_suggestion(
   357→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   358→) -> None:
   359→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   360→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   361→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   362→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   363→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   364→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   365→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   366→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   367→        print(
   368→            colorize(
   369→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   370→                "cyan",
   371→            )
   372→        )
   373→        print()
   374→        return
   375→
   376→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   377→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   378→    if not lowest_name:
   379→        return
   380→
   381→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   382→    lowest_kind = None
   383→    lowest_score = 101.0
   384→    lowest_issues = 0
   385→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   386→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   387→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   388→            if ds:
   389→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   390→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   391→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   392→            break
   393→    else:
   394→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   395→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   396→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   397→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   398→                lowest_issues = 0
   399→                break
   400→
   401→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   402→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   403→            print(
   404→                colorize(
   405→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   406→                    "cyan",
   407→                )
   408→            )
   409→            print()
   410→            return
   411→
   412→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   413→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   414→        if target_dim:
   415→            impact = 0.0
   416→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   417→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   418→                    {
   419→                        k: {
   420→                            "score": v["score"],
   421→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   422→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   423→                        }
   424→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   425→                        if "score" in v
   426→                    },
   427→                    potentials,
   428→                    det,
   429→                    lowest_issues,
   430→                )
   431→                if impact > 0:
   432→                    break
   433→
   434→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   435→            print(
   436→                colorize(
   437→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   438→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   439→                    "cyan",
   440→                )
   441→            )
   442→            print()
   443→
   444→
   445→def show_subjective_followup(
   446→    state: dict,
   447→    dim_scores: dict,
   448→    *,
   449→    target_strict_score: float,
   450→    objective_backlog: int = 0,

   453→    if not dim_scores:

   465→        max_integrity_items=5,
   466→    )
   467→    if print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=objective_backlog):

   501→def _collect_structural_areas(
   502→    state: dict,
   503→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   504→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   505→
   506→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   507→    """
   508→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   509→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   510→    )
   511→
   512→    structural = [
   513→        f
   514→        for f in findings.values()
   515→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   516→    ]
   517→
   518→    if len(structural) < 5:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   522→    for f in structural:
   523→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   524→        areas[area].append(f)
   525→
   526→    if len(areas) < 2:
   527→        return None
   528→
   529→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   530→
   531→
   532→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   533→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   534→    rows = []
   535→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   536→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   537→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   538→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   539→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   540→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   541→        rows.append(
   542→            [
   543→                area,
   544→                str(len(area_findings)),
   545→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   546→                str(open_count),
   547→                str(debt_count),
   548→                str(weight),
   549→            ]
   550→        )
   551→    return rows
   552→
   553→
   554→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   555→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   556→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   557→    if remaining > 0:
   558→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   559→
   560→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   561→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   562→    print(
   563→        colorize(
   564→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   565→        )
   566→    )
   567→    print(
   568→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   569→    )
   570→    print()
   571→
   572→
   573→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   574→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   575→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   576→    if sorted_areas is None:
   577→        return
   578→
   579→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   580→    print(
   581→        colorize(
   582→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   583→            "dim",
   584→        )
   585→    )
   586→    print()
   587→
   588→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   589→    print_table(
   590→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   591→    )
   592→
   593→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   594→
   595→
   596→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   597→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   598→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   599→    review_open = [
   600→        f
   601→        for f in findings.values()
   602→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   603→    ]
   604→    if not review_open:
   605→        return
   606→    uninvestigated = sum(
   607→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   608→    )
   609→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   610→    if uninvestigated:
   611→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   612→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   613→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   614→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   615→        print(
   616→            colorize(
   617→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   618→                "dim",
   619→            )
   620→        )
   621→    print()
   622→

   624→__all__ = [
   625→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
   626→    "print_scan_completeness",
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    80→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    81→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    # Primary source: subjective_assessments with placeholder=True
    86→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    87→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    88→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    89→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    90→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    91→                continue
    92→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    93→                continue
    94→            if dim_key:
    95→                unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    96→        return unscored
    97→
    98→    # Fallback: check dimension_scores directly for placeholder subjective
    99→    # dimensions.  This handles the common case where subjective_assessments
   100→    # hasn't been populated yet but dimension_scores already has placeholder
   101→    # entries from scan.  We can't use scorecard_subjective_entries() here
   102→    # because the scorecard pipeline intentionally hides placeholders.
   103→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   104→    if not dim_scores:
   105→        return set()
   106→
   107→    unscored = set()
   108→    for _name, data in dim_scores.items():
   109→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   110→            continue
   111→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   112→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   113→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   114→            continue
   115→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   116→            continue
   117→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   118→        if dim_key:
   119→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   120→    return unscored
   121→
   122→
   123→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   124→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→
   127→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   128→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   129→
   130→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   131→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   132→    """
   133→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   134→    if not promoted:
   135→        return 0
   136→    last_idx = -1
   137→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   138→        if fid in promoted:
   139→            last_idx = i
   140→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   141→
   142→
   143→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   145→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146→
   147→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   148→    plan: PlanModel,
   149→    state: StateModel,
   150→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   151→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   152→
   153→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   154→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   155→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   156→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   157→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   158→    """
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   161→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   164→
   165→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   166→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   167→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   168→        fid for fid in order
   169→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   170→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→    ]
   173→    for fid in to_remove:
   174→        order.remove(fid)
   175→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   176→
   177→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   178→    existing = set(order)
   179→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   180→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   181→        if uid not in existing:
   182→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   183→            result.injected.append(uid)
   184→
   185→    return result
   186→
   187→
   188→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   189→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→
   192→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   193→    plan: PlanModel,
   194→    state: StateModel,
   195→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   196→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   197→
   198→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   199→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   200→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   201→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   202→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   203→    """
   204→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   205→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   206→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   207→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   208→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   209→
   210→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   211→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   212→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   213→        fid for fid in order
   214→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   215→        and fid not in stale_ids
   216→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   217→    ]
   218→    for fid in to_remove:
   219→        order.remove(fid)
   220→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   221→
   222→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   223→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   224→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   225→        existing = set(order)
   226→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   227→            if sid not in existing:
   228→                order.append(sid)
   229→                result.injected.append(sid)
   230→
   231→    return result
   232→
   233→
   234→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   235→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   236→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   237→
   238→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   239→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   240→
   241→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   242→    """
   243→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   244→    review_ids = sorted(
   245→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   246→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   247→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   248→    )
   249→    if not review_ids:
   250→        return ""
   251→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   252→
   253→
   254→@dataclass
   255→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   256→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   257→
   258→    injected: bool = False
   259→    pruned: bool = False
   260→
   261→    @property
   262→    def changes(self) -> int:
   263→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   264→
   265→
   266→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   267→    plan: PlanModel,
   268→    state: StateModel,
   269→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   270→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   271→
   272→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   273→    """
   274→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   275→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   276→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   277→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   278→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   279→
   280→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   281→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   282→
   283→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject after promoted items
   284→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   285→        order.insert(_after_promoted(order, plan), SYNTHESIS_ID)
   286→        result.injected = True
   287→
   288→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   289→
   290→    return result
   291→
   292→
   293→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   294→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   295→
   296→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   297→    """
   298→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   299→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   300→    current = {
   301→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   302→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   303→    }
   304→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   305→
   306→
   307→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   308→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   309→
   310→    Returns True when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue OR the
   311→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   312→    """
   313→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   314→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   315→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   316→        return True
   317→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   318→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   319→    if not last_hash:
   320→        return False
   321→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   322→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   323→
   324→
   325→__all__ = [
   326→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   327→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   328→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   329→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   330→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   331→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   332→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   333→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   334→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   335→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   336→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   337→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   338→]
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueueBreakdown:
    17→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    18→
    19→    queue_total: int = 0
    20→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    21→    skipped: int = 0
    22→    subjective: int = 0
    23→    suppressed: int = 0
    24→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    25→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    26→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    27→
    28→
    29→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    plan: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    33→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    34→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    35→
    36→    result = build_work_queue(
    37→        state,
    38→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    39→            status="open",
    40→            count=None,
    41→            plan=plan,
    42→            collapse_clusters=True,
    43→            include_skipped=False,
    44→        ),
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    queue_total = result["total"]
    48→    suppressed = int(result.get("suppressed_count", 0))
    49→
    50→    # Count subjective items in the queue
    51→    subjective = sum(
    52→        1 for item in result.get("items", [])
    53→        if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    54→    )
    55→
    56→    # Plan-derived counts
    57→    plan_ordered = 0
    58→    skipped = 0
    59→    if plan:
    60→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    61→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    62→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    63→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    64→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    65→
    66→    # Focus cluster info
    67→    focus_cluster = None
    68→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    69→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    70→    if plan:
    71→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    72→        if active:
    73→            focus_cluster = active
    74→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    75→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    76→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    77→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    78→            open_findings = {
    79→                fid
    80→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    81→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    82→            }
    83→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    84→
    85→    return QueueBreakdown(
    86→        queue_total=queue_total,
    87→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    88→        skipped=skipped,
    89→        subjective=subjective,
    90→        suppressed=suppressed,
    91→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    92→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    93→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→
    97→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
    99→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100→
   101→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   102→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   103→
   104→    Examples::
   105→
   106→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   107→        Queue: 1934 items
   108→    """
   109→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   110→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   111→
   112→    # Parenthesized segments
   113→    segments: list[str] = []
   114→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   115→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   116→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   117→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   118→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   119→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   120→    if breakdown.suppressed > 0:
   121→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.suppressed} suppressed")
   122→
   123→    if segments:
   124→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   125→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   126→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   127→    return label
   128→
   129→
   130→def format_queue_block(
   131→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   132→    *,
   133→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   134→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   135→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   136→
   137→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   138→    """
   139→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   140→
   141→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   142→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   143→        focus_line = (
   144→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   145→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   146→            f" items remaining"
   147→        )
   148→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   149→
   150→    # Frozen score line
   151→    if frozen_score is not None:
   152→        lines.append((
   153→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   154→            "cyan",
   155→        ))
   156→
   157→    # Queue headline — always the same
   158→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   159→
   160→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   161→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   162→        lines.append((
   163→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   164→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   165→            "dim",
   166→        ))
   167→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   168→        lines.append((
   169→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   170→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   171→            "dim",
   172→        ))
   173→    else:
   174→        lines.append((
   175→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        ))
   178→
   179→    return lines
   180→
   181→
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   184→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   185→
   186→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   187→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   188→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   189→
   190→    result = build_work_queue(
   191→        state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            status="open",
   194→            count=None,
   195→            plan=plan,
   196→            collapse_clusters=True,
   197→            include_skipped=False,
   198→        ),
   199→    )
   200→    return result["total"]
   201→
   202→
   203→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   204→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   205→    if not plan:
   206→        return None
   207→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   208→
   209→
   210→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   211→    plan: dict,
   212→    queue_remaining: int,
   213→    *,
   214→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   215→) -> None:
   216→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   217→
   218→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   219→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   220→    """
   221→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   222→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   223→    if strict is None:
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    if breakdown is not None:
   227→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   228→        print()
   229→        for text, style in block:
   230→            print(colorize(text, style))
   231→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   232→            print(colorize(
   233→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   234→                "dim",
   235→            ))
   236→        return
   237→
   238→    # Legacy fallback
   239→    print(
   240→        colorize(
   241→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   242→            "cyan",
   243→        )
   244→    )
   245→    print(
   246→        colorize(
   247→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   248→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   249→            "dim",
   250→        )
   251→    )
   252→
   253→
   254→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   255→    state: dict,
   256→    prev,
   257→    plan: dict | None,
   258→) -> None:
   259→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   260→
   261→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
   262→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
   263→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
   264→    """
   265→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   266→        try:
   267→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   268→            remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   269→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   270→            breakdown = None
   271→            remaining = 0
   272→        if remaining > 0 and breakdown is not None:
   273→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   274→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   275→            )
   276→            return
   277→
   278→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
   279→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   280→
   281→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   282→
   283→
   284→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   285→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   286→
   287→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   288→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   289→    """
   290→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   291→
   292→    try:
   293→        plan = load_plan()
   294→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   295→        plan = None
   296→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   297→
   298→
   299→__all__ = [
   300→    "QueueBreakdown",
   301→    "format_queue_block",
   302→    "format_queue_headline",
   303→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   304→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   305→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   306→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   307→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   308→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   309→]
   310→
```

```
1→"""Shared synthesis guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
     9→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    compute_new_finding_ids,
    13→    is_synthesis_stale,
    14→    load_plan,
    15→    synthesis_phase_banner,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    21→    """Structured result from synthesis staleness detection."""
    22→
    23→    is_stale: bool = False
    24→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    25→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    26→
    27→
    28→def synthesis_guardrail_status(
    29→    *,
    30→    plan: dict | None = None,
    31→    state: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    33→    """Pure detection: is synthesis stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    34→    try:
    35→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    36→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    37→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult()
    38→
    39→    resolved_state = state or {}
    40→
    41→    if not is_synthesis_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    42→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    43→
    44→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    45→    if resolved_state:
    46→        new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    47→
    48→    return SynthesisGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_synthesis_guardrail_info(
    52→    *,
    53→    plan: dict | None = None,
    54→    state: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> bool:
    56→    """Print yellow info banner if synthesis is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    57→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    58→    if not result.is_stale:
    59→        return False
    60→
    61→    if result.new_ids:
    62→        print(colorize(
    63→            f"  {len(result.new_ids)} new review finding(s) not yet triaged.",
    64→            "yellow",
    65→        ))
    66→
    67→    if result._plan is not None:
    68→        banner = synthesis_phase_banner(result._plan)
    69→        if banner:
    70→            print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    71→
    72→    return True
    73→
    74→
    75→def require_synthesis_current_or_exit(
    76→    *,
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    bypass: bool = False,
    79→    attest: str = "",
    80→) -> None:
    81→    """Gate: exit(1) if synthesis is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
    82→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(state=state)
    83→    if not result.is_stale:
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
    87→        print(colorize(
    88→            "  Synthesis guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
    89→            "yellow",
    90→        ))
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    94→    print(colorize(
    95→        f"\n  BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review finding(s) have not been triaged.",
    96→        "red",
    97→    ))
    98→    if new_ids:
    99→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   100→            f = state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {})
   101→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}", "red"))
   102→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   104→    print()
   105→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP: desloppify plan synthesize", "yellow"))
   106→    print(colorize(
   107→        "  (Review new findings, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)",
   108→        "dim",
   109→    ))
   110→    print()
   111→    print(colorize("  View new items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   112→    print(colorize(
   113→        '  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."',
   114→        "dim",
   115→    ))
   116→    print()
   117→    sys.exit(1)
   118→
   119→
   120→__all__ = [
   121→    "SynthesisGuardrailResult",
   122→    "print_synthesis_guardrail_info",
   123→    "require_synthesis_current_or_exit",
   124→    "synthesis_guardrail_status",
   125→]
   126→
```

```
1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(
    21→    followup: SubjectiveFollowup,
    22→    *,
    23→    leading_newline: bool = False,
    24→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
    25→) -> bool:
    26→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    27→
    28→    When *objective_backlog* > 0, quality nudges are suppressed entirely — the
    29→    queue already surfaces subjective work when it becomes actionable.
    30→    Integrity warnings are always shown (anti-gaming safeguard).
    31→
    32→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    33→    """
    34→    printed = False
    35→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    36→    if followup.low_assessed and objective_backlog <= 0:
    37→        print(
    38→            colorize(
    39→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    40→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    41→                "cyan",
    42→            )
    43→        )
    44→        print(
    45→            colorize(
    46→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    47→                "dim",
    48→            )
    49→        )
    50→        printed = True
    51→
    52→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    53→        if printed:
    54→            print()
    55→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    56→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    57→        printed = True
    58→    return printed
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]
    62→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   169→
   170→
   171→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   172→    """Build a synthetic T1 work item for ``synthesis::pending`` if it's in the queue.
   173→
   174→    Returns ``None`` when synthesis is not pending.
   175→    """
   176→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   177→
   178→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   182→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   183→
   184→    open_review_ids = {
   185→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   186→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   187→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   188→    }
   189→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   190→    new_since = len(open_review_ids - synthesized_ids)
   191→    resolved_since = len(synthesized_ids - open_review_ids)
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   195→        "tier": 1,
   196→        "confidence": "high",
   197→        "detector": "synthesis",
   198→        "file": ".",
   199→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   200→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   201→        "summary": (
   202→            f"Synthesize {len(open_review_ids)} review findings into a coherent plan"
   203→        ),
   204→        "detail": {
   205→            "total_review_findings": len(open_review_ids),
   206→            "new_since_last": new_since,
   207→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since,
   208→        },
   209→    }
   210→
   211→
   212→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   213→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   214→    if not dim_scores:
   215→        return {}
   216→
   217→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   218→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   219→    for entry in entries:
   220→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   221→        if not name:
   222→            continue
   223→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   224→        [REDACTED](name)
   225→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   226→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   227→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   228→            if not key:
   229→                continue
   230→            aliases.add(key)
   231→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   232→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   233→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   234→        for alias in aliases:
   235→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   236→    return scores
   237→
   238→
   239→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   240→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   241→
   242→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   243→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   244→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   245→    """
   246→    entries: list[dict] = []
   247→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   248→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   249→            continue
   250→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   251→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   252→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   253→            continue
   254→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   255→            continue
   256→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   257→        entries.append(
   258→            {
   259→                "name": name,
   260→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   261→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   262→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   263→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   264→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   265→                "placeholder": True,
   266→                "stale": False,
   267→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   268→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   269→            }
   270→        )
   271→    return entries
   272→
   273→
   274→def build_subjective_items(
   275→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   276→) -> list[dict]:
   277→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   278→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   279→    if not dim_scores:
   280→        return []
   281→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   282→
   283→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   284→
   285→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   286→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   287→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   288→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   289→        for e in subjective_entries
   290→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   291→    }
   292→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   293→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   294→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   295→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   296→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   297→
   298→    if not subjective_entries:
   299→        return []
   300→    unassessed_dims = {
   301→        str(name).strip()
   302→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   303→            dim_scores
   304→        )
   305→    }
   306→
   307→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   308→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   309→    for finding in findings.values():
   310→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   311→            continue
   312→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   313→        if not dim_key:
   314→            continue
   315→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   316→
   317→    items: list[dict] = []
   318→    def _prepare_command(
   319→        cli_keys: list[str],
   320→        *,
   321→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   322→    ) -> str:
   323→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   324→        if cli_keys:
   325→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   326→        if force_review_rerun:
   327→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   328→        return command
   329→
   330→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   331→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   332→        if not name:
   333→            continue
   334→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   335→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   336→            continue
   337→
   338→        [REDACTED](name)
   339→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   340→        cli_keys = [
   341→            str(key).strip().lower()
   342→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   343→            if str(key).strip()
   344→        ]
   345→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   346→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   347→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   348→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   349→            name in unassessed_dims
   350→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   351→        )
   352→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   353→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   354→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   355→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   356→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   357→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   358→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   359→        if open_review > 0:
   360→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   361→        elif is_unassessed:
   362→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   363→        elif is_stale:
   364→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   365→                cli_keys,
   366→                force_review_rerun=True,
   367→            )
   368→        elif cli_keys:
   369→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   370→                cli_keys,
   371→                force_review_rerun=True,
   372→            )
   373→        else:
   374→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   375→                cli_keys,
   376→                force_review_rerun=True,
   377→            )
   378→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   379→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   380→        items.append(
   381→            {
   382→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   383→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   384→                "file": ".",
   385→                "confidence": "medium",
   386→                "summary": summary,
   387→                "detail": {
   388→                    "dimension_name": name,
   389→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   390→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   391→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   392→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   393→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   394→                },
   395→                "status": "open",
   396→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   397→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   398→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   399→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   400→            }
   401→        )
   402→    return items
   403→
```

```
1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel

    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→    plan: dict | None = None,
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps.
    19→
    20→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead
    21→    of the narrative-derived steps.
    22→    """
    23→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
    24→        _print_plan_agent_block(plan, header=header)
    25→        return
    26→    if not steps:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    29→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    31→    if next_command:
    32→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    38→        QueueBreakdown,
    39→        format_queue_headline,
    40→    )
    41→
    42→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    43→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    44→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    45→    skipped = len(skipped_ids)
    46→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    47→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    48→
    49→    # Build a lightweight breakdown for the headline
    50→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    51→        queue_total=ordered,
    52→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    53→        skipped=skipped,
    54→    )
    55→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    56→
    57→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    58→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {headline}", "dim"))
    59→    if active:
    60→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    61→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    62→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    63→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    64→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    65→
    66→
    67→def print_replacement_groups(
    68→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    69→    *,
    70→    title: str,
    71→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    72→) -> None:
    73→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    74→    if not groups:
    75→        return
    76→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    77→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    78→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    79→        for old, new in replacements:
    80→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    81→    print()
    82→
    83→
    84→def print_ranked_actions(
    85→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    86→) -> bool:
    87→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    88→    ranked = sorted(
    89→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    90→        key=lambda action: (
    91→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    92→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    93→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    94→        ),
    95→    )
    96→    if not ranked:
    97→        return False
    98→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    99→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
   100→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
   101→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
   102→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
   103→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
   104→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
   105→        if cluster_count:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
   107→        else:
   108→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
   109→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
   110→    return True
   111→
   112→
   113→__all__ = [
   114→    "print_agent_plan",
   115→    "print_ranked_actions",
   116→    "print_replacement_groups",
   117→]
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["review_weight"] = (
    56→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    57→        )
    58→        subjective_score = None
    59→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    60→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    61→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    62→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    63→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    64→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    65→            )
    66→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    67→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    68→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    69→            item,
    70→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    71→        )
    72→
    73→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    74→            continue
    75→        out.append(item)
    76→
    77→    return out
    78→
    79→
    80→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
    85→        # Synthesis items always sort first — they block all other work.
    86→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    89→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    90→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    91→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    92→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    93→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    94→        )
    95→        return (
    96→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
    97→            action_pri,
    98→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
    99→            item.get("id", ""),
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   103→        return (
   104→            1,
   105→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   106→            subjective_score_value(item),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   111→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   112→    return (
   113→        1,
   114→        0,
   115→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   116→        -review_weight,
   117→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   118→        item.get("id", ""),
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   123→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   124→        return {
   125→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   126→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   127→            "policy": (
   128→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   129→            ),
   130→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   131→        }
   132→
   133→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   134→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   135→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   136→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   137→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   138→    if is_subjective:
   139→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   140→    elif is_review:
   141→        ranking_factors = [
   142→            "confidence asc",
   143→            "review_weight desc",
   144→            "count desc",
   145→            "id asc",
   146→        ]
   147→    else:
   148→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   149→    explain = {
   150→        "kind": "finding",
   151→        "confidence": confidence,
   152→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   153→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   154→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   155→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   156→    }
   157→    if is_review:
   158→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   159→    if is_subjective:
   160→        explain["policy"] = (
   161→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   162→        )
   163→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   164→    return explain
   165→
   166→
   167→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   168→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   169→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   170→    for item in items:
   171→        if group == "file":
   172→            key = item.get("file", "")
   173→        elif group == "detector":
   174→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   175→        elif group == "cluster":
   176→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   177→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   178→        else:
   179→            key = "items"
   180→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   181→    return grouped
   182→
   183→
   184→__all__ = [
   185→    "build_finding_items",
   186→    "item_explain",
   187→    "item_sort_key",
   188→    "subjective_score_value",
   189→    "group_queue_items",
   190→]
   191→
```
