## Comprehensive Analysis: Queue Surfaces, Coaching, and Phase-Gating

### 1. What `build_work_queue` Returns

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

The `WorkQueueResult` TypedDict (line 43-50) declares:

```python
class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    items: list[dict]
    total: int
    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    suppressed_count: int
```

However, the actual return at line 396-402 also includes `new_ids`:

```python
return {
    "items": all_items,
    "total": total,
    "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
    "new_ids": new_ids,
    "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
}
```

The `new_ids` field (a `set[str]`) is present in the return value but is **not declared in `WorkQueueResult`**. This is a TypedDict discrepancy (not a runtime problem since it's a dict).

**Item kinds in the queue:**
There are four distinct `kind` values that can appear in `items`:
1. **`"finding"`** -- regular mechanical findings, built by `build_finding_items()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` (line 28-77)
2. **`"subjective_dimension"`** -- synthetic items for subjective dimensions below the threshold, built by `build_subjective_items()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (line 274-402)
3. **`"synthesis_needed"`** -- a synthetic T1 blocking item for `synthesis::pending`, built by `build_synthesis_item()` in helpers.py (line 171-209)
4. **`"cluster"`** -- meta-items that collapse auto-clusters, built by `_collapse_clusters()` in core.py (line 217-296)

**Pipeline within `build_work_queue` (line 299-402):**
1. Build finding items from state (`build_finding_items`)
2. Record `has_objective_backlog` = whether any finding items exist (line 325)
3. If subjective is included and status is open/all, build subjective items; suppress `backlog_gated` ones while objective backlog exists (line 343)
4. Inject synthesis item if plan demands it (line 349-352)
5. Sort all items by `item_sort_key` (line 354)
6. Apply plan ordering if plan exists (line 357-366)
7. Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (line 368-376)
8. Apply subjective interleave guardrail (3 objective : 1 subjective ratio) (line 378-386)
9. Slice to `count` (line 389-390)
10. Add explain metadata if requested (line 392-394)

### 2. How the `next` Command Consumes the Queue

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py`

`cmd_next` (line 70) is the entry point. The flow is:

1. **Pre-flight warnings** (lines 78-88): Tool staleness, skill version, config staleness, synthesis guardrail
2. **`_get_items`** (line 89) does the real work:
   - Loads the plan (line 185-193)
   - Resolves cluster focus (line 195-199)
   - Calls `build_work_queue()` (line 201-215)
   - Computes narrative via `compute_narrative()` (line 222-224)
   - Builds the JSON payload and writes to `query.json` (line 225-232)
   - If non-terminal output requested (JSON, markdown, file), emits and returns (line 234-235)
   - Extracts frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown (line 238-242)
   - **Renders the queue header** (line 244): `render_queue_header` shows "Queue: N items"
   - **Checks for empty queue** (line 246-252): `show_empty_queue` prints "Queue cleared!" with before/after comparison if plan_start_strict is available, or "Nothing to do!" otherwise
   - **Renders items** (line 261-265): `render_terminal_items` renders the actual cards
   - **Resolution hint** (line 266): `render_single_item_resolution_hint`
   - **Follow-up nudges** (line 267-276): `render_followup_nudges` -- the main coaching block

### 3. Where Coaching Messages Are Shown

Coaching is layered across multiple locations. Here is the complete map:

#### A. Pre-Command Banners (shown before queue content)

**Synthesis guardrail** -- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/guardrails.py`
- `print_synthesis_guardrail_info()` (line 51-72): Yellow info banner showing "N new review findings not yet triaged" and the synthesis phase banner. Called at the top of both `cmd_next` (line 88) and `cmd_status` (line 106).
- `require_synthesis_current_or_exit()` (line 75-117): Hard blocker that exits with code 1 if synthesis is stale and not bypassed. This is the strictest phase gate -- used by resolve/done commands (not next/status).

**Tool/skill/config staleness** -- Both `cmd_next` (lines 78-86) and `cmd_status` (lines 83-91) print yellow warnings for stale tools, stale SKILL.md, or stale config.

#### B. Empty Queue Coaching

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`, `show_empty_queue()` (line 345-370)
- When queue is empty and plan_start_strict is available: "Queue cleared!" with frozen-start vs. live-estimate comparison, plus "Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize"
- When queue is empty without plan context: "Nothing to do!" with strict score

#### C. Follow-up Nudges (after items)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`, `render_followup_nudges()` (line 467-613)

This is the richest coaching surface. It contains these coaching layers in order:

1. **Frozen score + queue progress block** (lines 494-517): When plan_start_strict is available and queue is not empty, shows the frozen score and queue breakdown with "Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`."

2. **North star score gap** (lines 518-533): When no frozen score, shows "strict X/100 -> target Y (+Z needed)" or "meets target"

3. **Queue block without frozen score** (lines 535-538): Shows `format_queue_block` when breakdown exists but no frozen score

4. **Subjective bottleneck banner** (lines 540-573): **Only shown when `_objective_remaining <= 0`**. This is a key phase-gating behavior. When the objective queue is clear, it computes whether subjective drag exceeds mechanical drag, and if so, surfaces "Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck" with the review re-run command. The comment on line 542 explicitly states the design intent: "While objective items remain, the queue is the single authority on what to work on next; no need to distract with subjective advice."

5. **Integrity lines** (lines 576-577): Always shown (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible per code comment).

6. **Rescan nudge** (lines 580-585): When queue_total > 10, shows tip about rescanning after structural fixes.

7. **Collapsed subjective summary** (lines 587-612): Shows "Subjective: N dimensions below target, N stale, N unassessed..." with pointer to `desloppify show subjective`.

#### D. Status Command Coaching

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py`

The status command has its own coaching surfaces:

1. **Frozen score + queue context** (lines 114-128): `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context` when plan is active with queue items
2. **Live score lines** (lines 130-137): When no frozen score
3. **Queue breakdown** (lines 138-147): Even without frozen score
4. **Dimension table** (line 158): `show_dimension_table` -- receives `objective_backlog` count
5. **Focus suggestion** (line 167): `show_focus_suggestion` -- shows lowest-scoring dimension or plan focus cluster
6. **Subjective followup** (lines 168-173): `show_subjective_followup` -- receives `objective_backlog` for gating
7. **Agent plan** (line 177): `show_agent_plan` -- either living plan block or narrative actions
8. **Headline** (lines 179-181): narrative headline

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render.py`

Key coaching in the status renderer:

- `_render_dimension_legend()` (line 290-324): Shows stale rerun command **only when `objective_backlog <= 0`** (line 315). Same phase-gating pattern.
- `show_subjective_followup()` (line 445-468): Delegates to `print_subjective_followup()` which gates quality nudges behind `objective_backlog <= 0` (see subjective.py line 36).

### 4. The Stale Dimension "Only After Queue Clears" Logic

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`

`sync_stale_dimensions()` (line 192-231) implements this:

```python
def sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state):
    # Step 1: Prune resolved subjective IDs (no longer stale AND not unscored)
    to_remove = [
        fid for fid in order
        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
        and fid not in stale_ids
        and fid not in unscored_ids
    ]
    # ... removes them ...

    # Step 2: Inject only when no objective items remain
    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
        # ... append stale IDs to end of queue ...
```

The critical check is on **line 223**: `has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))`. This checks the **plan's `queue_order`** list, not the live state. It considers any item in queue_order that does NOT have the `subjective::` prefix to be a "real" (objective) item.

**Only when `has_real_items` is False** (meaning the queue_order contains zero non-subjective items) does the function inject stale dimension IDs. The stale IDs are appended to the **end** of queue_order (line 228: `order.append(sid)`).

Contrast with `sync_unscored_dimensions()` (line 147-185): Unscored dimensions are injected **unconditionally** at the **front** of the queue (after promoted items), regardless of whether objective items remain. This is because initial reviews are onboarding priority.

**When is sync_stale_dimensions called?** It would be called during reconciliation (after scan save, referenced in the architecture notes as `scan_workflow.py`).

### 5. Phase-Gating Behavior Across the Codebase

There are three distinct phase-gating mechanisms:

| Gate | Where | Condition | Effect |
|------|-------|-----------|--------|
| Subjective suppression in queue builder | core.py:343 | `has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated")` | Backlog-gated subjective items (those whose only action is --force-review-rerun) are excluded from queue |
| Subjective nudge suppression in `next` render | render.py:547 | `_objective_remaining <= 0` | Subjective bottleneck banner only shown after all objective items cleared |
| Subjective followup gating in status | subjective.py:36 | `objective_backlog <= 0` | Quality nudges suppressed while objective backlog exists |
| Stale dimension injection in plan sync | stale_dimensions.py:223 | `not has_real_items` | Stale dimensions only injected into plan queue after all non-subjective items cleared |
| Stale rerun command in status dimension legend | status_parts/render.py:315 | `objective_backlog <= 0` | Stale rerun command only shown when no objective backlog |
| Synthesis hard gate | guardrails.py:75-117 | `is_synthesis_stale(plan, state)` | Blocks resolve/done commands entirely if synthesis is stale |

### 6. Where Would Be the Cleanest Place to Add Phase-Gating Behavior

Based on this analysis, the architecture already has a clear pattern for phase-gating. Here are the cleanest insertion points, depending on what you want to gate:

**A. If you want to gate what items appear in the queue itself:**

The cleanest place is in `build_work_queue()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`, between the item collection step and the sort/plan-order step (around lines 324-354). This is where the existing `has_objective_backlog` suppression lives (line 343). Any new phase gate that decides "don't show item type X until condition Y" belongs here. The `QueueBuildOptions` dataclass (line 26-41) is the natural place to add new gating configuration.

**B. If you want to gate what coaching/nudges are shown in the terminal:**

The `render_followup_nudges()` function in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` (line 467-613) is the single richest coaching surface. The existing `_objective_remaining` variable at line 543 already computes the objective backlog, and the subjective bottleneck banner at lines 547-573 demonstrates the pattern: check a condition, render coaching text. Adding a new coaching phase here follows the established pattern cleanly. The equivalent for status is `show_subjective_followup()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render.py` (line 445-468) with its `objective_backlog` parameter.

**C. If you want to gate what items the plan itself tracks:**

The `sync_stale_dimensions()` function in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` (line 192-231) is the model for plan-level phase gates. The pattern is: check a condition against `queue_order` contents, then conditionally inject/prune IDs. You'd add a sibling `sync_*` function following the same dataclass-result pattern (`StaleDimensionSyncResult`).

**D. If you want a hard gate that blocks commands:**

The `require_synthesis_current_or_exit()` pattern in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/guardrails.py` (line 75-117) is the template. It checks a condition, prints a structured error, and calls `sys.exit(1)`. New hard gates should live in this file as sibling functions.

**E. If you want the phase information to flow through to `query.json` for LLM consumption:**

The `build_query_payload()` function in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/output.py` (line 64-107) builds the structured payload. A new `"phase"` or `"phase_gate"` key could be added here, derived from the same conditions used for terminal rendering. The `_build_next_payload()` wrapper in `next.py` (line 106-127) is where state-derived fields like scores and dimensions are merged in.

**My recommendation for the cleanest single insertion point:** The `QueueBreakdown` dataclass in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py` (line 16-27) is already computed by both `next` and `status`, and it already carries `queue_total` and `subjective` counts. Adding a `phase` field (or `objective_remaining` field) to `QueueBreakdown` would make phase information available to all consumers without changing the queue builder itself. Both `render_followup_nudges` and `show_subjective_followup` already receive the breakdown object, so they could branch on the phase field naturally. The `format_queue_block` function already uses the breakdown for display, so phase-specific messaging would flow through the same path.

> DEVELOPER

Design an implementation plan for a phased onboarding flow in the desloppify work queue. Here's the context:

## Problem
The work queue needs to enforce a phased progression:
1. **Initial review phase**: When unassessed subjective dimensions exist (placeholders), ONLY show those items. Hide everything else until all initial reviews are complete.
2. **Plan-needed phase**: Once all initial reviews are done, nudge the user to run `desloppify plan` to build their prioritized queue.
3. **Working phase**: Normal queue — show everything.
4. **Stale review**: Only prompt to redo stale subjective dimensions AFTER the user has gone through all queue items (queue clears). This already works via existing code.

## Existing Code Context

### Queue builder: `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`
- `build_work_queue()` (line 299-402) is the central queue builder used by `next`, `status`, `plan`
- Returns `WorkQueueResult` TypedDict with `items`, `total`, `grouped`, `suppressed_count`, `new_ids`
- `QueueBuildOptions` dataclass configures the build
- Items are built, sorted, plan-ordered, cluster-collapsed, interleaved, then sliced to count
- Existing phase gate: `has_objective_backlog` suppresses `backlog_gated` subjective items (line 343)

### Subjective items: `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`
- `build_subjective_items()` creates synthetic queue items for subjective dimensions
- Items already tagged with `initial_review=True` for unassessed dimensions (fix already applied)
- `initial_review` items have `kind: "subjective_dimension"` and `primary_command` pointing to `desloppify review --prepare`

### Stale dimensions: `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`
- `sync_stale_dimensions()` only injects stale IDs when `has_real_items` is False (line 200)
- `sync_unscored_dimensions()` prepends unscored IDs to front of `queue_order`
- Both run after scan save in `scan_workflow.py`

### Rendering: `desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`
- `render_followup_nudges()` (line 467-613) is the main coaching surface after queue items
- Already has subjective bottleneck banner gated behind `_objective_remaining <= 0`
- `show_empty_queue()` (line 345-370) shows "Queue cleared!" or "Nothing to do!"

### Next command: `desloppify/app/commands/next.py`
- `_get_items()` builds the queue, computes narrative, writes query.json
- Passes queue result to render functions
- Shows coaching banners, queue header, items, resolution hints, follow-up nudges

### Status command: `desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py`
- Uses `build_work_queue()` for queue breakdown
- Has its own coaching surfaces that gate stale dimension nudges behind `objective_backlog <= 0`

## Requirements
1. The initial_review gate should ONLY apply when dimensions haven't been done yet (all placeholders). NOT when they're stale.
2. After initial reviews complete, prompt to run `desloppify plan` (the user needs to plan before seeing the full queue)
3. Stale review prompts only appear after queue clears — this already works
4. Should be "elegantly engineered" — clean phase detection, not scattered conditionals

## Design Questions to Address
1. Where should phase detection live? (queue builder vs. rendering vs. separate function)
2. Should `plan_needed` phase also gate the queue (hide items) or just show a nudge?
3. How to detect "plan needed" — e.g., no unassessed dims AND queue_order has no non-subjective items AND objective findings exist?
4. How to flow phase info to all consumers (next, status, plan commands)?
5. Should there be a `queue_phase` field in the return dict?

```
    57→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    *,
    60→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    61→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    62→) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    64→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    65→        return items
    66→
    67→    subjective_items = deque(
    68→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    69→    )
    70→    objective_items = deque(
    71→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    72→    )
    73→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    74→        return items
    75→
    76→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    77→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    78→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    79→            if not objective_items:
    80→                break
    81→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    82→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    83→            if not subjective_items:
    84→                break
    85→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    86→
    87→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    88→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    89→    return interleaved

   107→def _apply_plan_order(
   108→    items: list[dict],
   109→    plan: dict,
   110→    *,
   111→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   112→    cluster: str | None = None,
   113→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   114→) -> list[dict]:
   115→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   116→
   117→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   118→    2. Existing remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   119→    3. New items (from latest scan) sort after existing remaining items.
   120→    4. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   121→    5. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   122→    """
   123→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   124→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   125→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   126→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   127→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   128→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   129→
   130→    # Build lookup
   131→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   132→    for item in items:
   133→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   134→
   135→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   136→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   137→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   138→        if override.get("description"):
   139→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   140→        if override.get("note"):
   141→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   142→        if override.get("cluster"):
   143→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   144→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   145→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   146→                "name": cluster_name,
   147→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   148→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   149→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   150→            }
   151→
   152→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   153→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   154→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   155→    for fid in queue_order:
   156→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   157→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   158→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   159→
   160→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   161→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
   162→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
   163→    _new = new_ids or set()
   164→    for item in items:
   165→        item_id = item["id"]
   166→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   167→            continue
   168→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   169→            skipped_items.append(item)
   170→        elif item_id in _new:
   171→            remaining_new.append(item)
   172→        else:
   173→            remaining_existing.append(item)
   174→
   175→    # Assign queue positions — new items after existing ones
   176→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
   177→    if include_skipped:
   178→        result = result + skipped_items
   179→
   180→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   181→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   182→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   183→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   184→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   185→            if skip_entry:
   186→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   187→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   188→                if skip_reason:
   189→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   190→
   191→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   192→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   193→    if effective_cluster:
   194→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   195→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   196→        if cluster_member_ids:
   197→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   198→
   199→    return result

   202→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   203→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   204→    try:
   205→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   206→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   207→        if meta:
   208→            return meta.action_type
   209→    except ImportError as exc:
   210→        _ = exc
   211→    return "manual_fix"

   217→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   218→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   219→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   220→    if not clusters:
   221→        return items
   222→
   223→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   224→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   225→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   226→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   227→            continue
   228→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   229→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   230→
   231→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   232→        return items
   233→
   234→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   235→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   236→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   237→
   238→    for item in items:
   239→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   240→        if cname:
   241→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   242→        else:
   243→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   244→
   245→    # Build cluster meta-items
   246→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   247→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   248→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   249→        if len(members) < 2:
   250→            result.extend(members)
   251→            continue
   252→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   253→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   254→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   255→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   256→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   257→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   258→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   259→            action_type = "reorganize"
   260→        else:
   261→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   262→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   263→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   264→                action_type = "refactor"
   265→
   266→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   267→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   268→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   269→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   270→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   271→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   272→        else:
   273→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   274→
   275→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   276→        if not primary_command:
   277→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   278→
   279→        meta_item: dict = {
   280→            "id": cname,
   281→            "kind": "cluster",
   282→            "action_type": action_type,
   283→            "summary": summary,
   284→            "members": members,
   285→            "member_count": len(members),
   286→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   287→            "cluster_name": cname,
   288→            "cluster_auto": True,
   289→            "confidence": "high",
   290→            "detector": detector,
   291→            "file": "",
   292→        }
   293→        result.append(meta_item)
   294→
   295→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   296→    return result

   298→
   299→def build_work_queue(
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   303→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   304→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   305→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   306→
   307→    status = resolved_options.status
   308→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   309→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   310→    try:
   311→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   312→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   313→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   314→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   315→
   316→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   317→        state,
   318→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   319→        status_filter=status,
   320→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   321→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   322→    )
   323→
   324→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   325→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   326→    suppressed_count = 0
   327→    if (
   328→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   329→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   330→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   331→    ):
   332→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   333→            state,
   334→            state.get("findings", {}),
   335→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   336→        )
   337→        for item in subjective_items:
   338→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   339→                continue
   340→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   341→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   342→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   343→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   344→                suppressed_count += 1
   345→                continue
   346→            all_items.append(item)
   347→
   348→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   349→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   350→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   351→        if synth_item is not None:
   352→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   353→
   354→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   355→
   356→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   357→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   358→    if resolved_options.plan:
   359→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   360→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   361→            all_items,
   362→            resolved_options.plan,
   363→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   364→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   365→            new_ids=new_ids,
   366→        )
   367→
   368→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   369→    should_collapse = (
   370→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   371→        and resolved_options.plan
   372→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   373→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   374→    )
   375→    if should_collapse:
   376→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   377→
   378→    should_interleave = (
   379→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   380→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   381→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   382→        and not resolved_options.scope
   383→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   384→    )
   385→    if should_interleave:
   386→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   387→
   388→    total = len(all_items)
   389→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   390→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   391→
   392→    if resolved_options.explain:
   393→        for item in all_items:
   394→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   395→
   396→    return {
   397→        "items": all_items,
   398→        "total": total,
   399→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   400→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   401→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   402→    }
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = [
   406→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   407→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   408→    "WorkQueueResult",
   409→    "build_work_queue",
   410→    "group_queue_items",
   411→]
   412→
```

```
     2→

    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:

    52→    weight_by_confidence = {

    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→

    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:

    67→

    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")

    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")

   239→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   240→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   241→
   242→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which

   245→    """
   246→    entries: list[dict] = []
   247→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   248→        if not isinstance(data, dict):

   250→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   251→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   252→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   253→            continue
   254→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):

   256→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   257→        entries.append(
   258→            {
   259→                "name": name,
   260→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   261→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   262→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   263→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   264→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   265→                "placeholder": True,
   266→                "stale": False,

   268→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   269→            }

   273→
   274→def build_subjective_items(
   275→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   276→) -> list[dict]:
   277→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   278→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   279→    if not dim_scores:
   280→        return []
   281→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   282→
   283→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   284→
   285→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   286→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   287→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   288→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   289→        for e in subjective_entries
   290→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   291→    }
   292→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   293→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   294→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   295→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   296→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   297→
   298→    if not subjective_entries:
   299→        return []
   300→    unassessed_dims = {
   301→        str(name).strip()
   302→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   303→            dim_scores
   304→        )
   305→    }
   306→
   307→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   308→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   309→    for finding in findings.values():
   310→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   311→            continue
   312→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   313→        if not dim_key:
   314→            continue
   315→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   316→
   317→    items: list[dict] = []
   318→    def _prepare_command(
   319→        cli_keys: list[str],
   320→        *,
   321→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   322→    ) -> str:
   323→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   324→        if cli_keys:
   325→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   326→        if force_review_rerun:
   327→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   328→        return command
   329→
   330→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   331→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   332→        if not name:
   333→            continue
   334→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   335→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   336→            continue
   337→
   338→        [REDACTED](name)
   339→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   340→        cli_keys = [
   341→            str(key).strip().lower()
   342→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   343→            if str(key).strip()
   344→        ]
   345→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   346→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   347→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   348→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   349→            name in unassessed_dims
   350→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   351→        )
   352→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   353→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   354→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   355→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   356→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   357→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   358→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   359→        if open_review > 0:
   360→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   361→        elif is_unassessed:
   362→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   363→        elif is_stale:
   364→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   365→                cli_keys,
   366→                force_review_rerun=True,
   367→            )
   368→        elif cli_keys:
   369→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   370→                cli_keys,
   371→                force_review_rerun=True,
   372→            )
   373→        else:
   374→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   375→                cli_keys,
   376→                force_review_rerun=True,
   377→            )
   378→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   379→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   380→        items.append(
   381→            {
   382→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   383→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   384→                "file": ".",
   385→                "confidence": "medium",
   386→                "summary": summary,
   387→                "detail": {
   388→                    "dimension_name": name,
   389→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   390→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   391→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   392→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   393→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   394→                },
   395→                "status": "open",
   396→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   397→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   398→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   399→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   400→            }
   401→        )
   402→    return items
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    80→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    81→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    # Primary source: subjective_assessments with placeholder=True
    86→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    87→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    88→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    89→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    90→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    91→                continue
    92→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    93→                continue
    94→            if dim_key:
    95→                unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    96→        return unscored
    97→
    98→    # Fallback: check dimension_scores directly for placeholder subjective
    99→    # dimensions.  This handles the common case where subjective_assessments
   100→    # hasn't been populated yet but dimension_scores already has placeholder
   101→    # entries from scan.  We can't use scorecard_subjective_entries() here
   102→    # because the scorecard pipeline intentionally hides placeholders.
   103→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   104→    if not dim_scores:
   105→        return set()
   106→
   107→    unscored = set()
   108→    for _name, data in dim_scores.items():
   109→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   110→            continue
   111→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   112→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   113→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   114→            continue
   115→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   116→            continue
   117→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   118→        if dim_key:
   119→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   120→    return unscored
   121→
   122→
   123→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   124→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→
   127→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   128→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   129→
   130→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   131→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   132→    """
   133→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   134→    if not promoted:
   135→        return 0
   136→    last_idx = -1
   137→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   138→        if fid in promoted:
   139→            last_idx = i
   140→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   141→
   142→
   143→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   145→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146→
   147→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   148→    plan: PlanModel,
   149→    state: StateModel,
   150→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   151→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   152→
   153→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   154→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   155→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   156→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   157→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   158→    """
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   161→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   164→
   165→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   166→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   167→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   168→        fid for fid in order
   169→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   170→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→    ]
   173→    for fid in to_remove:
   174→        order.remove(fid)
   175→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   176→
   177→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   178→    existing = set(order)
   179→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   180→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   181→        if uid not in existing:
   182→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   183→            result.injected.append(uid)
   184→
   185→    return result
   186→
   187→
   188→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   189→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→
   192→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   193→    plan: PlanModel,
   194→    state: StateModel,
   195→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   196→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   197→
   198→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   199→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   200→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   201→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   202→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   203→    """
   204→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   205→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   206→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   207→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   208→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   209→
   210→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   211→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   212→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   213→        fid for fid in order
   214→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   215→        and fid not in stale_ids
   216→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   217→    ]
   218→    for fid in to_remove:
   219→        order.remove(fid)
   220→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   221→
   222→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   223→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   224→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   225→        existing = set(order)
   226→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   227→            if sid not in existing:
   228→                order.append(sid)
   229→                result.injected.append(sid)
   230→
   231→    return result
   232→
   233→
   234→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   235→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   236→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   237→
   238→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   239→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   240→
   241→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   242→    """
   243→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   244→    review_ids = sorted(
   245→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   246→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   247→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   248→    )
   249→    if not review_ids:
   250→        return ""
   251→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   252→
   253→
   254→@dataclass
   255→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   256→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   257→
   258→    injected: bool = False
   259→    pruned: bool = False
   260→
   261→    @property
   262→    def changes(self) -> int:
   263→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   264→
   265→
   266→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   267→    plan: PlanModel,
   268→    state: StateModel,
   269→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   270→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   271→
   272→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   273→    """
   274→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   275→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   276→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   277→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   278→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   279→
   280→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   281→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   282→
   283→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject after promoted items
   284→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   285→        order.insert(_after_promoted(order, plan), SYNTHESIS_ID)
   286→        result.injected = True
   287→
   288→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   289→
   290→    return result
   291→
   292→
   293→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   294→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   295→
   296→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   297→    """
   298→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   299→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   300→    current = {
   301→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   302→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   303→    }
   304→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   305→
   306→
   307→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   308→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   309→
   310→    Returns True when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue OR the
   311→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   312→    """
   313→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   314→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   315→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   316→        return True
   317→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   318→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   319→    if not last_hash:
   320→        return False
   321→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   322→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   323→
   324→
   325→__all__ = [
   326→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   327→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   328→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   329→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   330→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   331→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   332→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   333→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   334→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   335→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   336→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   337→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   338→]
   339→
```

```
   298→            print(
   299→                colorize(
   300→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   301→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   302→                    "cyan",
   303→                )
   304→            )
   305→    if explain:
   306→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   307→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   308→        base = (
   309→            f"ranked by confidence={confidence}, "
   310→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   311→        )
   312→
   313→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   314→        if dim_scores and detector:
   315→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   316→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   317→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   318→                base += (
   319→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   320→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   321→                )
   322→
   323→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   324→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   325→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   326→            if dim_key:
   327→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   328→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   329→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   330→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   331→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   332→                        break
   333→
   334→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   335→        if policy:
   336→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   337→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   338→
   339→
   340→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   341→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   342→    print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def show_empty_queue(
   346→    queue: dict,
   347→    strict: float | None,
   348→    *,
   349→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   350→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   351→) -> bool:
   352→    if queue.get("items"):
   353→        return False
   354→    # Show before/after comparison when plan_start_strict is available
   355→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   356→        delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
   357→        delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
   358→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   359→        print(colorize(
   360→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   361→            "cyan",
   362→        ))
   363→        print(colorize(
   364→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   365→            "dim",
   366→        ))
   367→    else:
   368→        suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   369→        print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   370→    return True
   371→
   372→
   373→def _render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   374→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   375→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   376→    tag = _effort_tag(item)
   377→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   378→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   379→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   380→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   381→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   382→
   383→
   384→def render_terminal_items(
   385→    items: list[dict],
   386→    dim_scores: dict,
   387→    findings_scoped: dict,
   388→    *,
   389→    group: str,
   390→    explain: bool,
   391→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   392→    plan: dict | None = None,
   393→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   394→) -> None:
   395→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   396→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   397→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   398→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   399→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   400→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   401→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   402→
   403→    if group != "item":
   404→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   405→        return
   406→
   407→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   408→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   409→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   410→    )
   411→
   412→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   413→        if idx > 0:
   414→            print()
   415→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   416→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   417→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   418→            continue
   419→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   420→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   421→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   422→        elif len(items) > 1:
   423→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   424→        else:
   425→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   426→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   427→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   428→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   429→
   430→
   431→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   432→    if len(items) != 1:
   433→        return
   434→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   435→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   436→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   437→        return
   438→    item = items[0]
   439→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   440→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   441→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   442→        primary = item.get(
   443→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   444→        )
   445→        print(f"    {primary}")
   446→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   447→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   448→        return
   449→
   450→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   451→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   452→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   453→        print(f"    {primary}")
   454→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   455→    else:
   456→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   457→
   458→    print(
   459→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   460→    )
   461→    print(
   462→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   463→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   464→    )
   465→
   466→
   467→def render_followup_nudges(
   468→    state: dict,
   469→    dim_scores: dict,
   470→    findings_scoped: dict,
   471→    *,
   472→    strict_score: float | None,
   473→    target_strict_score: float,
   474→    queue_total: int = 0,
   475→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   476→    breakdown: "QueueBreakdown | None" = None,
   477→) -> None:
   478→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   479→        QueueBreakdown,
   480→        format_queue_block,
   481→    )
   482→
   483→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   484→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   485→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   486→        state,
   487→        subjective_entries,
   488→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   489→        max_quality_items=3,
   490→        max_integrity_items=5,
   491→    )
   492→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   493→    # Show frozen plan-start score + queue block when in an active cycle
   494→    if queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None and breakdown is not None:
   495→        frozen = plan_start_strict
   496→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen)
   497→        print()
   498→        for text, style in block:
   499→            print(colorize(text, style))
   500→        print(colorize(
   501→            "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   502→            "dim",
   503→        ))
   504→    elif queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None:
   505→        print(
   506→            colorize(
   507→                f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100",
   508→                "cyan",
   509→            )
   510→        )
   511→        print(
   512→            colorize(
   513→                f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   514→                " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   515→                "dim",
   516→            )
   517→        )
   518→    elif strict_score is not None:
   519→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   520→        if gap > 0:
   521→            print(
   522→                colorize(
   523→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   524→                    "cyan",
   525→                )
   526→            )
   527→        else:
   528→            print(
   529→                colorize(
   530→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   531→                    "green",
   532→                )
   533→            )
   534→    # Show queue block after north star when no frozen score
   535→    if breakdown is not None and queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is None:
   536→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   537→        for text, style in block:
   538→            print(colorize(text, style))
   539→
   540→    # Subjective bottleneck banner — only shown when the objective queue is
   541→    # clear.  While objective items remain, the queue is the single authority
   542→    # on what to work on next; no need to distract with subjective advice.
   543→    _objective_remaining = max(
   544→        0,
   545→        (breakdown.queue_total - breakdown.subjective) if breakdown else queue_total,
   546→    )
   547→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores and _objective_remaining <= 0:
   548→        try:
   549→            health_breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   550→            subjective_drag = sum(
   551→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   552→                for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   553→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   554→            )
   555→            mechanical_drag = sum(
   556→                float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   557→                for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   558→                if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   559→            )
   560→            if subjective_drag > mechanical_drag and subjective_drag > 5.0:
   561→                print(colorize(
   562→                    f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   563→                    f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   564→                    "yellow",
   565→                ))
   566→                print(colorize(
   567→                    "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   568→                    "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   569→                    "to re-score.",
   570→                    "yellow",
   571→                ))
   572→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   573→            log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   574→
   575→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   576→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   577→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   578→
   579→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   580→    if queue_total > 10:
   581→        print(colorize(
   582→            "\n  Tip: after structural fixes (splitting files, moving code), rescan to "
   583→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   584→            "dim",
   585→        ))
   586→
   587→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   588→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   589→        findings_scoped
   590→    )
   591→    parts: list[str] = []
   592→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   593→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   594→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   595→    open_review = [
   596→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   597→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   598→    ]
   599→    if low_dims:
   600→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   601→    if stale_count:
   602→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   603→    if unassessed_count:
   604→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   605→    if len(open_review):
   606→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   607→    if coverage_open > 0:
   608→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   609→
   610→    if parts:
   611→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   612→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   613→
   614→
   615→__all__ = [
   616→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   617→    "render_followup_nudges",
   618→    "render_queue_header",
   619→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   620→    "render_terminal_items",
   621→    "scorecard_subjective",
   622→    "show_empty_queue",
   623→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   624→]
   625→
```

```
    38→def _scorecard_subjective(
    39→    state: dict,
    40→    dim_scores: dict,
    41→) -> list[dict]:
    42→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    43→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
    68→

    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    72→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    73→    state = runtime.state
    74→    config = runtime.config
    75→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    79→    if stale_warning:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    81→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    82→    if skill_warning:
    83→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    84→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    85→    if config_warning:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    87→
    88→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(state=state)
    89→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    90→

    92→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    93→    plan_data: dict | None,
    94→    *,
    95→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    96→    scope: str | None,
    97→) -> str | None:
    98→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    99→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   100→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   101→        if active_cluster:
   102→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   103→    return effective_cluster

   116→    )

   124→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [

   173→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   174→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   175→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   176→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   177→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")

   181→

   183→

   186→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   187→    if (

   192→        plan_data = plan

   195→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   196→        plan_data,

   198→        scope=scope,
   199→    )
   200→
   201→    queue = build_work_queue(
   202→        state,
   203→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   204→            count=count,
   205→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   206→            scope=scope,
   207→            status=status,
   208→            include_subjective=True,
   209→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   210→            explain=explain,
   211→            plan=plan_data,
   212→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   213→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   214→        ),
   215→    )
   216→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   217→
   218→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   219→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   220→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   221→        state,
   222→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   223→    )
   224→
   225→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   226→        queue=queue,
   227→        items=items,
   228→        state=state,
   229→        narrative=narrative,
   230→        plan_data=plan_data,
   231→    )
   232→    write_query(payload)
   233→
   234→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   235→        return
   236→
   237→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   238→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   239→        state=state,
   240→        plan_data=plan_data,
   241→    )
   242→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   243→
   244→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   245→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   246→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   247→        queue,
   248→        strict_score,
   249→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   250→        target_strict=target_strict,
   251→    ):
   252→        return
   253→
   254→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   255→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   256→        state.get("findings", {}),
   257→        state.get("scan_path"),
   258→    )
   259→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   260→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   261→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   262→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   263→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   264→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   265→    )
   266→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   267→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   268→        state,
   269→        dim_scores,
   270→        findings_scoped,
   271→        strict_score=strict_score,
   272→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   273→        queue_total=queue_total,
   274→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   275→        breakdown=breakdown,
   276→    )
   277→    print()
   278→
   279→
   280→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   281→
```

```
   238→        "suppression": suppression,
   239→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   240→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
```
