1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueueBreakdown:
    17→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    18→
    19→    queue_total: int = 0
    20→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    21→    skipped: int = 0
    22→    subjective: int = 0
    23→    suppressed: int = 0
    24→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    25→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    26→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    27→
    28→
    29→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    plan: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    33→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    34→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    35→
    36→    result = build_work_queue(
    37→        state,
    38→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    39→            status="open",
    40→            count=None,
    41→            plan=plan,
    42→            collapse_clusters=True,
    43→            include_skipped=False,
    44→        ),
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    queue_total = result["total"]
    48→    suppressed = int(result.get("suppressed_count", 0))
    49→
    50→    # Count subjective items in the queue
    51→    subjective = sum(
    52→        1 for item in result.get("items", [])
    53→        if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    54→    )
    55→
    56→    # Plan-derived counts
    57→    plan_ordered = 0
    58→    skipped = 0
    59→    if plan:
    60→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    61→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    62→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    63→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    64→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    65→
    66→    # Focus cluster info
    67→    focus_cluster = None
    68→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    69→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    70→    if plan:
    71→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    72→        if active:
    73→            focus_cluster = active
    74→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    75→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    76→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    77→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    78→            open_findings = {
    79→                fid
    80→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    81→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    82→            }
    83→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    84→
    85→    return QueueBreakdown(
    86→        queue_total=queue_total,
    87→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    88→        skipped=skipped,
    89→        subjective=subjective,
    90→        suppressed=suppressed,
    91→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    92→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    93→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→
    97→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
    99→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100→
   101→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   102→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   103→
   104→    Examples::
   105→
   106→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   107→        Queue: 1934 items
   108→    """
   109→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   110→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   111→
   112→    # Parenthesized segments
   113→    segments: list[str] = []
   114→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   115→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   116→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   117→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   118→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   119→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   120→    if breakdown.suppressed > 0:
   121→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.suppressed} suppressed")
   122→
   123→    if segments:
   124→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   125→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   126→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   127→    return label
   128→
   129→
   130→def format_queue_block(
   131→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   132→    *,
   133→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   134→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   135→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   136→
   137→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   138→    """
   139→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   140→
   141→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   142→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   143→        focus_line = (
   144→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   145→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   146→            f" items remaining"
   147→        )
   148→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   149→
   150→    # Frozen score line
   151→    if frozen_score is not None:
   152→        lines.append((
   153→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   154→            "cyan",
   155→        ))
   156→
   157→    # Queue headline — always the same
   158→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   159→
   160→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   161→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   162→        lines.append((
   163→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   164→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   165→            "dim",
   166→        ))
   167→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   168→        lines.append((
   169→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   170→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   171→            "dim",
   172→        ))
   173→    else:
   174→        lines.append((
   175→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        ))
   178→
   179→    return lines
   180→
   181→
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   184→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   185→
   186→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   187→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   188→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   189→
   190→    result = build_work_queue(
   191→        state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            status="open",
   194→            count=None,
   195→            plan=plan,
   196→            collapse_clusters=True,
   197→            include_skipped=False,
   198→        ),
   199→    )
   200→    return result["total"]
   201→
   202→
   203→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   204→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   205→    if not plan:
   206→        return None
   207→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   208→
   209→
   210→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   211→    plan: dict,
   212→    queue_remaining: int,
   213→    *,
   214→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   215→) -> None:
   216→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   217→
   218→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   219→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   220→    """
   221→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   222→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   223→    if strict is None:
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    if breakdown is not None:
   227→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   228→        print()
   229→        for text, style in block:
   230→            print(colorize(text, style))
   231→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   232→            print(colorize(
   233→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   234→                "dim",
   235→            ))
   236→        return
   237→
   238→    # Legacy fallback
   239→    print(
   240→        colorize(
   241→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   242→            "cyan",
   243→        )
   244→    )
   245→    print(
   246→        colorize(
   247→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   248→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   249→            "dim",
   250→        )
   251→    )
   252→
   253→
   254→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   255→    state: dict,
   256→    prev,
   257→    plan: dict | None,
   258→) -> None:
   259→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   260→
   261→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
   262→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
   263→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
   264→    """
   265→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   266→        try:
   267→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   268→            remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   269→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   270→            breakdown = None
   271→            remaining = 0
   272→        if remaining > 0 and breakdown is not None:
   273→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   274→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   275→            )
   276→            return
   277→
   278→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
   279→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   280→
   281→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   282→
   283→
   284→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   285→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   286→
   287→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   288→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   289→    """
   290→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   291→
   292→    try:
   293→        plan = load_plan()
   294→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   295→        plan = None
   296→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   297→
   298→
   299→__all__ = [
   300→    "QueueBreakdown",
   301→    "format_queue_block",
   302→    "format_queue_headline",
   303→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   304→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   305→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   306→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   307→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   308→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   309→]
   310→

    22→
    23→
    24→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    25→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    26→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    27→
    28→
    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    74→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    75→    reset_plan(plan)
    76→    append_log_entry(
    77→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    78→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    79→    )
    80→    save_plan(plan)
    81→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    82→
    83→
    84→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    85→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    86→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    87→
    88→    if plan_action is None:
    89→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    if plan_action == "show":
    93→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    if plan_action == "queue":
    97→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "reset":
   101→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "move":
   105→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action in (
   109→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   110→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   111→    ):
   112→        dispatch = {
   113→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   114→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   115→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   116→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   117→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   118→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   119→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   120→        }
   121→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   125→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   129→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   130→
   131→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   135→
   136→
   137→# Backwards-compatible alias
   138→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   141→

    84→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    85→    """Return a banner string when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue."""
    86→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    87→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    88→        return ""
    89→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    90→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    91→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
    92→    if completed:
    93→        return (
    94→            f"Synthesis in progress ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete). "
    95→            "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    96→        )
    97→    return (
    98→        "Synthesis needed — review findings require analysis before fixing. "
    99→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
   100→    )

    70→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):

    74→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    75→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    76→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    77→        return
    78→    try:
    79→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    80→        _save_config(config)
    81→    except OSError as exc:
    82→        _ = exc
    83→        config["needs_rescan"] = True
    84→
    85→
    86→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    87→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    88→    return bool(
    89→        plan.get("queue_order")
    90→        or plan.get("overrides")
    91→        or plan.get("clusters")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    97→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    98→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    99→        return False
   100→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)

    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",
    99→            "overall_score": overall_score,
   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   101→            "strict_score": strict_score,
   102→            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_score,
   103→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   104→            "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   105→            "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   106→            "stats": stats,
   107→            "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,
   110→            "ignores": ignores,
   111→            "suppression": suppression,
   112→            "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   113→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   114→            "open_scope": open_scope,
   115→            "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),
   117→            "narrative": narrative,
   118→            **({"plan": {
   119→                "active": True,
   120→                "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   121→                "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   122→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   124→            }} if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped")) else {}),
   125→        }
   126→    )

   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   132→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   133→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))
   134→
   135→    last_ignored = int(suppression.get("last_ignored", 0) or 0)
   136→    last_raw = int(suppression.get("last_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   137→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   138→
   139→    if last_raw > 0:
   140→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   144→                style,
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   148→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   149→
   150→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"    Recent ({recent_scans} scans): {recent_ignored}/{recent_raw} findings hidden ({recent_pct:.1f}%)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )

   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,

   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _render_dimension_legend(
   291→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   292→    state: dict | None = None,
   293→    *,
   294→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
   295→) -> None:
   296→    """Print the legend footer and, when actionable, the stale rerun command."""
   297→    print(
   298→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   299→    )
   300→    print(
   301→        colorize(
   302→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   303→            "dim",
   304→        )
   305→    )
   306→    stale_keys = [
   307→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   308→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   309→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   310→    ]
   311→    if stale_keys:
   312→        print(
   313→            colorize("  [stale] = assessment outdated", "yellow")
   314→        )
   315→        if objective_backlog <= 0:
   316→            n = len(stale_keys)
   317→            dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   318→            print(
   319→                colorize(
   320→                    f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   321→                    f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg} --force-review-rerun`",
   322→                    "yellow",
   323→                )
   324→            )
   325→
   326→
   327→def show_dimension_table(
   328→    state: dict, dim_scores: dict, *, objective_backlog: int = 0,
   329→) -> None:
   330→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   331→    print()
   332→    bar_len = 20
   333→    print(
   334→        colorize(
   335→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",

   344→
   345→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   346→    _render_subjective_dimensions(

   348→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   349→        bar_len=bar_len,
   350→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   351→    )
   352→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state, objective_backlog=objective_backlog)

   355→
   356→def show_focus_suggestion(
   357→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   358→) -> None:
   359→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   360→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   361→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   362→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   363→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   364→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   365→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   366→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   367→        print(
   368→            colorize(
   369→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   370→                "cyan",
   371→            )
   372→        )
   373→        print()
   374→        return
   375→
   376→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   377→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   378→    if not lowest_name:
   379→        return
   380→
   381→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   382→    lowest_kind = None
   383→    lowest_score = 101.0
   384→    lowest_issues = 0
   385→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   386→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   387→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   388→            if ds:
   389→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   390→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   391→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   392→            break
   393→    else:
   394→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   395→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   396→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   397→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   398→                lowest_issues = 0
   399→                break
   400→
   401→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   402→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   403→            print(
   404→                colorize(
   405→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   406→                    "cyan",
   407→                )
   408→            )
   409→            print()
   410→            return
   411→
   412→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   413→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   414→        if target_dim:
   415→            impact = 0.0
   416→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   417→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   418→                    {
   419→                        k: {
   420→                            "score": v["score"],
   421→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   422→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   423→                        }
   424→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   425→                        if "score" in v
   426→                    },
   427→                    potentials,
   428→                    det,
   429→                    lowest_issues,
   430→                )
   431→                if impact > 0:
   432→                    break
   433→
   434→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   435→            print(
   436→                colorize(
   437→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   438→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   439→                    "cyan",
   440→                )
   441→            )
   442→            print()
   443→
   444→

   501→def _collect_structural_areas(
   502→    state: dict,
   503→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   504→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   505→
   506→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   507→    """
   508→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   509→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   510→    )
   511→
   512→    structural = [
   513→        f
   514→        for f in findings.values()
   515→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   516→    ]
   517→
   518→    if len(structural) < 5:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   522→    for f in structural:
   523→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   524→        areas[area].append(f)
   525→
   526→    if len(areas) < 2:
   527→        return None
   528→
   529→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   530→
   531→
   532→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   533→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   534→    rows = []
   535→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   536→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   537→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   538→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   539→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   540→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   541→        rows.append(
   542→            [
   543→                area,
   544→                str(len(area_findings)),
   545→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   546→                str(open_count),
   547→                str(debt_count),
   548→                str(weight),
   549→            ]
   550→        )
   551→    return rows
   552→
   553→
   554→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   555→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   556→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   557→    if remaining > 0:
   558→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   559→
   560→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   561→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   562→    print(
   563→        colorize(
   564→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   565→        )
   566→    )
   567→    print(
   568→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   569→    )
   570→    print()
   571→
   572→
   573→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   574→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   575→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   576→    if sorted_areas is None:
   577→        return
   578→
   579→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   580→    print(
   581→        colorize(
   582→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   583→            "dim",
   584→        )
   585→    )
   586→    print()
   587→
   588→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   589→    print_table(
   590→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   591→    )
   592→
   593→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   594→
   595→
   596→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   597→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   598→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   599→    review_open = [
   600→        f
   601→        for f in findings.values()
   602→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   603→    ]
   604→    if not review_open:
   605→        return
   606→    uninvestigated = sum(
   607→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   608→    )
   609→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   610→    if uninvestigated:
   611→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   612→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   613→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   614→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   615→        print(
   616→            colorize(
   617→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   618→                "dim",
   619→            )
   620→        )
   621→    print()

    46→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    47→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    48→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    49→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    50→
    51→
    52→class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    53→    timestamp: Required[str]
    54→    action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
    55→    finding_ids: list[str]
    56→    cluster_name: str | None
    57→    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"
    58→    note: str | None
    59→    detail: dict  # action-specific extra data
    60→
    61→
    62→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    63→    original_id: Required[str]
    64→    original_detector: str
    65→    original_file: str
    66→    original_summary: str
    67→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    68→    superseded_at: str
    69→    remapped_to: str | None
    70→    candidates: list[str]
    71→    note: str | None
    72→
    73→
    74→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    75→    version: Required[int]
    76→    created: Required[str]
    77→    updated: Required[str]
    78→    queue_order: list[str]
    79→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    80→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    81→    active_cluster: str | None
    82→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    83→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    84→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    85→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
    86→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
    87→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
    88→    epic_synthesis_meta: dict  # synthesis engine metadata
    89→
    90→
    91→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    92→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    93→    now = utc_now()
    94→    return {
    95→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    96→        "created": now,
    97→        "updated": now,
    98→        "queue_order": [],
    99→        "deferred": [],
   100→        "skipped": {},
   101→        "active_cluster": None,
   102→        "overrides": {},
   103→        "clusters": {},
   104→        "superseded": {},
   105→        "promoted_ids": [],
   106→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   107→        "execution_log": [],
   108→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   109→    }
   110→
   111→
   112→def _ensure_container_types(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   113→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   114→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   115→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   116→        plan["deferred"] = []
   117→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   118→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   119→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   120→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   121→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   122→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   123→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   124→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   125→    if not isinstance(plan.get("promoted_ids"), list):
   126→        plan["promoted_ids"] = []
   127→    if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict):
   128→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   129→    if not isinstance(plan.get("execution_log"), list):
   130→        plan["execution_log"] = []
   131→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   132→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   133→
   134→
   135→def _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   136→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   137→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   138→    if not deferred:
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    now = utc_now()
   142→    for fid in list(deferred):
   143→        if fid in skipped:
   144→            continue
   145→        skipped[fid] = {
   146→            "finding_id": fid,
   147→            "kind": "temporary",
   148→            "reason": None,
   149→            "note": None,
   150→            "attestation": None,
   151→            "created_at": now,
   152→            "review_after": None,
   153→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   154→        }
   155→    deferred.clear()
   156→
   157→
   158→def _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   159→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   160→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   161→            continue
   162→        if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   163→            cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   164→        cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   165→        cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   166→        cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   167→        cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   171→    """Migrate v3 top-level ``epics`` dict into ``clusters`` (v4 unification)."""
   172→    epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   173→    if not isinstance(epics, dict) or not epics:
   174→        return
   175→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   176→    now = utc_now()
   177→    for name, epic in epics.items():
   178→        if not isinstance(epic, dict):
   179→            continue
   180→        if name in clusters:
   181→            continue  # don't overwrite existing cluster
   182→        clusters[name] = {
   183→            "name": name,
   184→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   185→            "finding_ids": epic.get("finding_ids", []),
   186→            "auto": True,
   187→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
   188→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
   189→            "user_modified": False,
   190→            "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
   191→            "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
   192→            # Epic-specific fields
   193→            "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   194→            "direction": epic.get("direction", "simplify"),
   195→            "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   196→            "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
   197→            "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
   198→            "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   199→            "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   200→            "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   201→            "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
   202→            "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   203→            "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   204→        }
   205→
   206→
   207→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   208→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   209→
   210→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   211→    and v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters).
   212→    """
   213→    defaults = empty_plan()
   214→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   215→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   216→
   217→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   218→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   219→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   220→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   221→
   222→
   223→def synthesis_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   224→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   225→    return {
   226→        name: cluster
   227→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   228→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   229→    }
   230→
   231→
   232→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   233→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   234→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   235→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   236→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   237→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   238→
   239→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   240→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   241→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   242→    if overlap:
   243→        raise ValueError(
   244→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   245→        )
   246→
   247→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   248→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   249→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   250→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   251→            raise ValueError(
   252→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   253→            )
   254→
   255→
   256→__all__ = [
   257→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   258→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   259→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   260→    "Cluster",
   261→    "ItemOverride",
   262→    "PlanModel",
   263→    "SkipEntry",
   264→    "SupersededEntry",
   265→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   266→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   267→    "empty_plan",
   268→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   269→    "synthesis_clusters",
   270→    "validate_plan",
   271→]
   272→

   378→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   379→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   380→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   381→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   382→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   383→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   384→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   385→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   386→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   387→            payload.pop("components", None)
   388→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   389→            continue
   390→        assessments[key] = {
   391→            "score": 0.0,
   392→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   393→            "assessed_at": now,
   394→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   395→            "placeholder": True,
   396→        }

   400→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   401→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   402→) -> int:
   403→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   404→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   405→
   406→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   407→    expired = 0
   408→    for payload in assessments.values():
   409→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   410→            continue
   411→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   412→            continue
   413→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   414→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   415→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   416→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   417→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   418→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   419→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   420→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   421→        payload.pop("components", None)
   422→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   423→        expired += 1
   424→    return expired

   428→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   429→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   430→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   431→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   432→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)

   435→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   436→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   437→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   438→        state
   439→    )
   440→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   441→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   442→            state,
   443→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   444→        )
   445→
   446→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   447→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   448→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   449→
   450→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   451→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   452→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   453→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   454→
   455→    return ScanRuntime(
   456→        args=args,
   457→        state_path=state_file,
   458→        state=state,
   459→        path=path,
   460→        config=config,
   461→        lang=lang,
   462→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   463→        profile=profile,
   464→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   465→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   466→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   467→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   468→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   469→    )
   470→
   471→
   472→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   473→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   474→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   475→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   476→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   477→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   478→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   479→        return findings
   480→
   481→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   482→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   483→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   484→    )
   485→    if not stale:
   486→        return findings
   487→
   488→    augmented = list(findings)
   489→    augmented.extend(stale)
   490→    for stale_finding in stale:
   491→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   492→    return augmented
   493→
   494→
   495→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   496→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   497→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   498→    *,
   499→    decay_scans: int,
   500→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   501→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   502→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   503→        findings,
   504→        state=runtime.state,
   505→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   506→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   507→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   508→    )
   509→
   510→
   511→def run_scan_generation(
   512→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   513→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   514→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   515→    enable_file_cache()
   516→    enable_parse_cache()
   517→    try:
   518→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   519→            runtime.path,
   520→            lang=runtime.lang,
   521→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   522→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   523→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   524→                profile=runtime.profile,
   525→            ),
   526→        )
   527→    finally:
   528→        disable_parse_cache()
   529→        disable_file_cache()
   530→
   531→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   532→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   533→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   534→    )
   535→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   536→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   537→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   538→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   539→    )
   540→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   541→        findings,
   542→        runtime,
   543→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   544→    )
   545→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   546→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   547→
   548→
   549→def merge_scan_results(
   550→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   551→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   552→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   553→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   554→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   555→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   556→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   557→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   558→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   559→
   560→    if not path_changed:
   561→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   562→    else:
   563→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   564→    prev_dim_scores = (
   565→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   566→    )
   567→
   568→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   569→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   570→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   571→
   572→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   573→
   574→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   575→        runtime.state,
   576→        findings,
   577→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   578→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   579→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   580→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   581→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   582→            potentials=potentials,
   583→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   584→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   585→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   586→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   587→        ),
   588→    )
   589→
   590→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   591→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   592→    )
   593→    state_mod.save_state(
   594→        runtime.state,
   595→        runtime.state_path,
   596→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   597→    )
   598→
   599→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   600→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   601→
   602→    return ScanMergeResult(
   603→        diff=diff,
   604→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   605→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   606→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   607→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   608→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   609→    )
   610→
   611→
   612→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   613→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   614→    config: dict[str, object],
   615→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   616→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   617→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   618→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   619→    )
   620→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   621→    open_findings = [
   622→        finding
   623→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   624→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   625→        ).values()
   626→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   627→    ]
   628→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   629→        open_findings,
   630→        budget=noise_budget,
   631→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   632→    )
   633→
   634→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   635→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   636→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   637→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   638→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   639→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   640→    )
   641→
   642→
   643→def persist_reminder_history(
   644→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   645→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   646→) -> None:
   647→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   648→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   649→        return
   650→
   651→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   652→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   653→    state_mod.save_state(
   654→        runtime.state,
   655→        runtime.state_path,
   656→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   657→    )
   658→
   659→
   660→__all__ = [
   661→    "ScanStateContractError",
   662→    "ScanMergeResult",
   663→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   664→    "ScanRuntime",
   665→    "merge_scan_results",
   666→    "persist_reminder_history",
   667→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   668→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   669→    "run_scan_generation",
   670→]
   671→

1→"""Shared synthesis guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
     9→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    compute_new_finding_ids,
    13→    is_synthesis_stale,
    14→    load_plan,
    15→    synthesis_phase_banner,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    21→    """Structured result from synthesis staleness detection."""
    22→
    23→    is_stale: bool = False
    24→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    25→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    26→
    27→
    28→def synthesis_guardrail_status(
    29→    *,
    30→    plan: dict | None = None,
    31→    state: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> SynthesisGuardrailResult:
    33→    """Pure detection: is synthesis stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    34→    try:
    35→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    36→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    37→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult()
    38→
    39→    resolved_state = state or {}
    40→
    41→    if not is_synthesis_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    42→        return SynthesisGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    43→
    44→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    45→    if resolved_state:
    46→        new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    47→
    48→    return SynthesisGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_synthesis_guardrail_info(
    52→    *,
    53→    plan: dict | None = None,
    54→    state: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> bool:
    56→    """Print yellow info banner if synthesis is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    57→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    58→    if not result.is_stale:
    59→        return False
    60→
    61→    if result.new_ids:
    62→        print(colorize(
    63→            f"  {len(result.new_ids)} new review finding(s) not yet triaged.",
    64→            "yellow",
    65→        ))
    66→
    67→    if result._plan is not None:
    68→        banner = synthesis_phase_banner(result._plan)
    69→        if banner:
    70→            print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    71→
    72→    return True
    73→
    74→
    75→def require_synthesis_current_or_exit(
    76→    *,
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    bypass: bool = False,
    79→    attest: str = "",
    80→) -> None:
    81→    """Gate: exit(1) if synthesis is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
    82→    result = synthesis_guardrail_status(state=state)
    83→    if not result.is_stale:
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
    87→        print(colorize(
    88→            "  Synthesis guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
    89→            "yellow",
    90→        ))
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    94→    print(colorize(
    95→        f"\n  BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review finding(s) have not been triaged.",
    96→        "red",
    97→    ))
    98→    if new_ids:
    99→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   100→            f = state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {})
   101→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}", "red"))
   102→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   104→    print()
   105→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP: desloppify plan synthesize", "yellow"))
   106→    print(colorize(
   107→        "  (Review new findings, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)",
   108→        "dim",
   109→    ))
   110→    print()
   111→    print(colorize("  View new items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   112→    print(colorize(
   113→        '  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."',
   114→        "dim",
   115→    ))
   116→    print()
   117→    sys.exit(1)
   118→
   119→
   120→__all__ = [
   121→    "SynthesisGuardrailResult",
   122→    "print_synthesis_guardrail_info",
   123→    "require_synthesis_current_or_exit",
   124→    "synthesis_guardrail_status",
   125→]
   126→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["review_weight"] = (
    56→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    57→        )
    58→        subjective_score = None
    59→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    60→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    61→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    62→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    63→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    64→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    65→            )
    66→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    67→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    68→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    69→            item,
    70→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    71→        )
    72→
    73→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    74→            continue
    75→        out.append(item)
    76→
    77→    return out
    78→
    79→
    80→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
    85→        # Synthesis items always sort first — they block all other work.
    86→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    89→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    90→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    91→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    92→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    93→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    94→        )
    95→        return (
    96→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
    97→            action_pri,
    98→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
    99→            item.get("id", ""),
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   103→        return (
   104→            1,
   105→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   106→            subjective_score_value(item),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   111→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   112→    return (
   113→        1,
   114→        0,
   115→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   116→        -review_weight,
   117→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   118→        item.get("id", ""),
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   123→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   124→        return {
   125→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   126→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   127→            "policy": (
   128→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   129→            ),
   130→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   131→        }
   132→
   133→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   134→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   135→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   136→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   137→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   138→    if is_subjective:
   139→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   140→    elif is_review:
   141→        ranking_factors = [
   142→            "confidence asc",
   143→            "review_weight desc",
   144→            "count desc",
   145→            "id asc",
   146→        ]
   147→    else:
   148→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   149→    explain = {
   150→        "kind": "finding",
   151→        "confidence": confidence,
   152→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   153→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   154→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   155→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   156→    }
   157→    if is_review:
   158→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   159→    if is_subjective:
   160→        explain["policy"] = (
   161→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   162→        )
   163→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   164→    return explain
   165→
   166→
   167→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   168→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   169→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   170→    for item in items:
   171→        if group == "file":
   172→            key = item.get("file", "")
   173→        elif group == "detector":
   174→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   175→        elif group == "cluster":
   176→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   177→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   178→        else:
   179→            key = "items"
   180→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   181→    return grouped
   182→
   183→
   184→__all__ = [
   185→    "build_finding_items",
   186→    "item_explain",
   187→    "item_sort_key",
   188→    "subjective_score_value",
   189→    "group_queue_items",
   190→]
   191→
