```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    35→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    36→        "file": item.get("file"),
    37→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    38→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    39→        "status": item.get("status"),
    40→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    41→    }
    42→    explain = item.get("explain")
    43→    if explain is not None:
    44→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    45→
    46→    # Plan metadata
    47→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    48→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    49→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    50→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    52→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    54→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    56→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    57→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    58→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    59→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    60→
    61→    return serialized
    62→
    63→
    64→def build_query_payload(
    65→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    66→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    67→    *,
    68→    command: str,
    69→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    70→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    71→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    72→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    73→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    74→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
    75→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    76→        "suppressed_count": queue.get("suppressed_count", 0),
    77→    }
    78→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    79→        "command": command,
    80→        "items": serialized,
    81→        "queue": queue_section,
    82→        "narrative": narrative,
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    if plan and (
    86→        plan.get("queue_order")
    87→        or plan.get("skipped")
    88→        or plan.get("clusters")
    89→    ):
    90→        clusters_summary = []
    91→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    92→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    93→            clusters_summary.append({
    94→                "name": name,
    95→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
    96→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
    97→            })
    98→        payload["plan"] = {
    99→            "active": True,
   100→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   101→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   102→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   103→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   104→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   105→        }
   106→
   107→    return payload
   108→
   109→
   110→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   111→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   112→    lines = [
   113→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   114→        "",
   115→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   116→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   117→    ]
   118→    for item in items:
   119→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   120→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   121→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   122→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   123→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   124→    lines.append("")
   125→    return "\n".join(lines)
   126→
   127→
   128→def write_output_file(
   129→    output_file: str,
   130→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   131→    item_count: int,
   132→    *,
   133→    safe_write_text_fn,
   134→    colorize_fn,
   135→) -> bool:
   136→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   137→    try:
   138→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   139→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   140→    except OSError as exc:
   141→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   142→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   143→        return False
   144→    return True
   145→
   146→
   147→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   148→    output_format: str,
   149→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   150→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   151→) -> bool:
   152→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   153→    renderers = {
   154→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   155→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   156→    }
   157→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   158→    if renderer is None:
   159→        return False
   160→    renderer()
   161→    return True
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "build_query_payload",
   166→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   167→    "render_markdown",
   168→    "serialize_item",
   169→    "write_output_file",
   170→]
   171→
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    79→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    80→
    81→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    82→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    84→        if subtype:
    85→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    86→
    87→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    88→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    89→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    90→
    91→    # Everything else → by detector
    92→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    93→
    94→
    95→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    96→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    97→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    98→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    99→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   100→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   101→    # detector::security → auto/security
   102→    parts = key.split("::")
   103→    if len(parts) == 2:
   104→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   105→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   106→        if prefix_type == "review":
   107→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   108→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   109→    if len(parts) == 3:
   110→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   111→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   112→
   113→
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# Description and action generation
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→def _generate_description(
   119→    cluster_name: str,
   120→    members: list[dict],
   121→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   122→    subtype: str | None,
   123→) -> str:
   124→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   125→    count = len(members)
   126→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   127→
   128→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   129→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   130→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   131→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   132→
   133→    if detector == "structural":
   134→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   135→        if len(files) == 1:
   136→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   137→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   138→
   139→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   140→    if subtype:
   141→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   142→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   143→
   144→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   145→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   146→
   147→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   148→
   149→
   150→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   151→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   152→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   153→        return None
   154→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   155→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   156→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   157→        return fixer_name
   158→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   159→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   160→        if subtype in fixer:
   161→            return fixer
   162→    return None
   163→
   164→
   165→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   166→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   167→
   168→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   169→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   170→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   171→    """
   172→    if " — " in guidance:
   173→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   174→    return guidance
   175→
   176→
   177→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   178→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   179→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   180→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   181→}
   182→
   183→
   184→def _generate_action(
   185→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   186→    subtype: str | None,
   187→) -> str:
   188→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   189→
   190→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   191→    """
   192→    if meta is None:
   193→        return "review and fix each finding"
   194→
   195→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   196→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   197→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   198→        if matched_fixer:
   199→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   200→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   201→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   202→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   203→
   204→    if meta.tool == "move":
   205→        return "desloppify move"
   206→
   207→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   208→    if meta.guidance:
   209→        if subtype:
   210→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   211→        return meta.guidance
   212→
   213→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   214→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   215→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   216→    )
   217→
   218→
   219→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   220→# Repair
   221→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   222→
   223→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
   224→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
   225→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   226→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   227→    repaired = 0
   228→    for override in overrides.values():
   229→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
   230→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
   231→            override["cluster"] = None
   232→            override["updated_at"] = now
   233→            repaired += 1
   234→    return repaired
   235→
   236→
   237→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   238→# Main algorithm
   239→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   240→
   241→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   242→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   243→
   244→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   245→    """
   246→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   247→    changes = 0
   248→
   249→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   250→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   251→
   252→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   253→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   254→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   255→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   256→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   257→
   258→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   259→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   260→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   261→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   262→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   263→            continue
   264→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   265→            continue
   266→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   267→            continue
   268→
   269→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   270→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   271→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   272→        if key is None:
   273→            continue
   274→
   275→        groups[key].append(fid)
   276→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   277→
   278→    # Drop singleton groups
   279→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   280→
   281→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   282→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   283→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   284→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   285→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   286→            if ck:
   287→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   288→
   289→    now = utc_now()
   290→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   291→
   292→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   293→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   294→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   295→
   296→        # Representative finding for metadata
   297→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   298→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   299→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   300→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   301→
   302→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   303→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   304→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   305→
   306→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   307→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   308→
   309→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   310→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   311→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   312→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   313→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   314→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   315→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   316→                if new_ids:
   317→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   318→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   319→                    changes += 1
   320→            else:
   321→                # Replace membership wholesale
   322→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   323→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   324→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   325→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   326→                    cluster["description"] = description
   327→                    cluster["action"] = action
   328→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   329→                    changes += 1
   330→        else:
   331→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   332→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   333→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   334→                # Append a disambiguator
   335→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   336→
   337→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   338→                "name": cluster_name,
   339→                "description": description,
   340→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   341→                "created_at": now,
   342→                "updated_at": now,
   343→                "auto": True,
   344→                "cluster_key": key,
   345→                "action": action,
   346→                "user_modified": False,
   347→            }
   348→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   349→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   350→            changes += 1
   351→
   352→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   353→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   354→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   355→        for fid in member_ids:
   356→            if fid not in overrides:
   357→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   358→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   359→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   360→
   361→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   362→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   363→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   364→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   365→    )
   366→    unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   367→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   368→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   369→    )
   370→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   371→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   372→    )
   373→
   374→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   375→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   376→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   377→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   378→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   379→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   380→
   381→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   382→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   383→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   384→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   385→            new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
   386→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   387→                cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
   388→                cluster["description"] = description
   389→                cluster["action"] = action
   390→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   391→                changes += 1
   392→        else:
   393→            clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
   394→                "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
   395→                "description": description,
   396→                "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
   397→                "created_at": now,
   398→                "updated_at": now,
   399→                "auto": True,
   400→                "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
   401→                "action": action,
   402→                "user_modified": False,
   403→            }
   404→            existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
   405→            changes += 1
   406→
   407→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   408→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
   409→        for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
   410→            if fid not in overrides:
   411→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   412→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   413→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   414→
   415→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   416→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   417→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   418→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   419→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   420→        action = (
   421→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   422→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   423→            + " --force-review-rerun"
   424→        )
   425→
   426→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   427→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   428→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   429→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   430→            new_ids = set(stale_queue_ids)
   431→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   432→                cluster["finding_ids"] = stale_queue_ids
   433→                cluster["description"] = description
   434→                cluster["action"] = action
   435→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   436→                changes += 1
   437→        else:
   438→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   439→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   440→                "description": description,
   441→                "finding_ids": stale_queue_ids,
   442→                "created_at": now,
   443→                "updated_at": now,
   444→                "auto": True,
   445→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   446→                "action": action,
   447→                "user_modified": False,
   448→            }
   449→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   450→            changes += 1
   451→
   452→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   453→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   454→        for fid in stale_queue_ids:
   455→            if fid not in overrides:
   456→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   457→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   458→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   459→
   460→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   461→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   462→        cluster = clusters[name]
   463→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   464→            continue
   465→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   466→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   467→            continue
   468→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   469→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   470→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   471→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   472→            if alive:
   473→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   474→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   475→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   476→                    changes += 1
   477→                continue
   478→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   479→        # Delete stale cluster
   480→        del clusters[name]
   481→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   482→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   483→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   484→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   485→                override["cluster"] = None
   486→                override["updated_at"] = now
   487→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   488→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   489→        changes += 1
   490→
   491→    # Repair any orphaned override cluster references from prior bugs
   492→    repaired = _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   493→    changes += repaired
   494→
   495→    plan["updated"] = now
   496→    return changes
   497→
   498→
   499→__all__ = [
   500→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   501→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   502→]
   503→
```

```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import re
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
    10→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    11→    "path_scoped_findings",
    12→    "is_ignored",

    14→    "remove_ignored_findings",

    18→
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    20→    Finding,
    21→    StateModel,
    22→    ensure_state_defaults,
    23→    utc_now,
    24→    validate_state_invariants,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel

    28→
    29→def path_scoped_findings(
    30→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    33→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    34→    return {
    35→        finding_id: finding
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    37→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    42→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    43→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    44→        return True
    45→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    46→    return (
    47→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    48→        or file_path == scan_path
    49→        or file_path == "."
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→def open_scope_breakdown(
    54→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    55→    scan_path: str | None,
    56→    *,
    57→    detector: str | None = None,
    58→) -> dict[str, int]:
    59→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    60→    in_scope = 0
    61→    out_of_scope = 0
    62→
    63→    for finding in findings.values():
    64→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    65→            continue
    66→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    67→            continue
    68→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    69→            continue
    70→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    71→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    72→            in_scope += 1
    73→        else:
    74→            out_of_scope += 1
    75→
    76→    return {
    77→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    78→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    79→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    84→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    85→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    86→
    87→
    88→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    89→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    90→) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    92→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    93→        if "*" in pattern:
    94→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    95→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    96→                return pattern
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        if "::" in pattern:
   100→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   101→                return pattern
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   105→            return pattern
   106→
   107→    return None
   108→
   109→
   110→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   111→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   112→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   113→    matched_ids = [
   114→        finding_id
   115→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   116→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   117→    ]
   118→    now = utc_now()
   119→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   120→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   121→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   122→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   123→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   124→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   125→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   126→
   127→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   128→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   129→    return len(matched_ids)
   130→
   131→
   132→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   133→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   134→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   135→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   136→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   137→    if pattern not in ignores:
   138→        ignores.append(pattern)
   139→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   140→
   141→
   142→def make_finding(
   143→    detector: str,
   144→    file: str,
   145→    name: str,
   146→    *,
   147→    tier: int,
   148→    confidence: str,
   149→    summary: str,
   150→    detail: dict | None = None,
   151→) -> Finding:
   152→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   153→    rfile = rel(file)
   154→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    return {
   157→        "id": finding_id,
   158→        "detector": detector,
   159→        "file": rfile,
   160→        "tier": tier,
   161→        "confidence": confidence,
   162→        "summary": summary,
   163→        "detail": detail or {},
   164→        "status": "open",
   165→        "note": None,
   166→        "first_seen": now,
   167→        "last_seen": now,
   168→        "resolved_at": None,
   169→        "reopen_count": 0,
   170→    }
   171→
   172→
   173→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   178→    if finding_id == pattern:
   179→        return True
   180→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   181→        return True
   182→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   183→        return True
   184→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   185→        return True
   186→    if (
   187→        finding.get("detector") == pattern
   188→        or finding["file"] == pattern
   189→        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   190→    ):
   191→        return True
   192→
   193→    # Name-segment fallback: bare name matches the last ::segment of the ID
   194→    if "::" not in pattern and "::" in finding_id:
   195→        segments = finding_id.split("::")
   196→        name_segment = segments[-1]
   197→        if name_segment == pattern:
   198→            return True
   199→        # For hashed IDs, also match the descriptive name (second-to-last segment)
   200→        if len(segments) >= 3 and _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment) and segments[-2] == pattern:
   201→            return True
   202→
   203→    return False
   204→
```

Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the entire system. Let me design the implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Phased Onboarding Flow in the Work Queue

### Architecture Overview

The core idea is to introduce a **`QueuePhase` enum** that represents the current onboarding state, compute it once in a pure function, flow it through the existing `WorkQueueResult` TypedDict, and let the queue builder and rendering layers both react to it. This keeps phase detection centralized and avoids scattered conditionals.

### Phase Definitions

```python
# New enum
class QueuePhase(str, Enum):
    INITIAL_REVIEW = "initial_review"   # Unassessed subjective dims exist
    PLAN_NEEDED = "plan_needed"         # Reviews done, no plan built yet
    WORKING = "working"                 # Normal queue with plan
    STALE_REVIEW = "stale_review"       # Queue cleared, stale dims surfaced
```

### Design Decisions (Answering the Questions)

**1. Where should phase detection live?**

In a new pure function `detect_queue_phase()` inside `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`. It belongs in the queue engine layer -- not in rendering, not in commands. The function takes `state`, `plan`, and `has_objective_findings` as inputs and returns a `QueuePhase`. This keeps the logic testable, centralized, and available to all consumers.

**2. Should `plan_needed` phase gate the queue (hide items) or just show a nudge?**

Just a nudge. The `INITIAL_REVIEW` phase should actively filter the queue to show only subjective review items. But `PLAN_NEEDED` should not hide items -- the user can still use `next` without a plan; we just strongly encourage planning. Hiding items at the plan_needed stage would be hostile to users who want to jump in. The gate only applies to the initial review phase where the user truly has no context yet.

**3. How to detect "plan needed"?**

The condition is:
- No unassessed (placeholder) subjective dimensions exist (initial reviews are complete)
- AND the plan has no meaningful queue_order (no non-subjective IDs in `queue_order`)  
- AND objective findings exist (there is work to plan)
- AND no `plan_start_scores` are set (no plan cycle has begun)

This detects the gap between "you finished your initial reviews" and "you ran `desloppify plan` to build your prioritized queue."

**4. How to flow phase info to all consumers?**

Add a `phase` key to `WorkQueueResult`. Since `WorkQueueResult` is a TypedDict, adding `phase: str` is straightforward. The phase string is then available everywhere the queue result is used -- `next`, `status`, `plan`, `query.json`.

**5. Should there be a `queue_phase` field in the return dict?**

Yes, as `phase` in `WorkQueueResult`. Also propagate into the `query.json` payload so external tools (SKILL.md, agent integrations) can react to the phase.

---

### Step-by-Step Implementation

#### Step 1: Add `QueuePhase` enum and `detect_queue_phase()` function

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`**

Add at the top of the module:

```python
from enum import Enum

class QueuePhase(str, Enum):
    """Onboarding phase of the work queue."""
    INITIAL_REVIEW = "initial_review"
    PLAN_NEEDED = "plan_needed"
    WORKING = "working"
    STALE_REVIEW = "stale_review"
```

Add the detection function (pure, no side effects):

```python
def detect_queue_phase(
    *,
    has_unassessed: bool,
    has_objective_findings: bool,
    plan: dict | None,
) -> QueuePhase:
    """Determine the current onboarding phase of the work queue.
    
    Phase progression:
    1. INITIAL_REVIEW — unassessed subjective dimensions exist (placeholders).
       The queue is gated to show only those items.
    2. PLAN_NEEDED — initial reviews complete, but no plan built yet.
       Show a nudge to run ``desloppify plan``.
    3. WORKING — normal queue operation.
    4. STALE_REVIEW — queue cleared, stale dimensions surfaced.
       (Already handled by existing stale_dimensions sync; included for completeness.)
    """
    if has_unassessed:
        return QueuePhase.INITIAL_REVIEW
    
    if has_objective_findings and plan is not None:
        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
        has_plan_content = any(
            fid for fid in queue_order
            if not fid.startswith("subjective::")
            and not fid.startswith("synthesis::")
        )
        has_plan_cycle = bool(plan.get("plan_start_scores"))
        
        if not has_plan_content and not has_plan_cycle:
            return QueuePhase.PLAN_NEEDED
    
    # Check for stale review: no objective findings left, only subjective stale items
    if not has_objective_findings and plan is not None:
        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
        only_stale_subjective = (
            bool(queue_order)
            and all(fid.startswith("subjective::") for fid in queue_order)
        )
        if only_stale_subjective:
            return QueuePhase.STALE_REVIEW
    
    return QueuePhase.WORKING
```

The key inputs are:
- `has_unassessed`: derived from the subjective items built in `build_work_queue()` (check `item.get("initial_review")`)
- `has_objective_findings`: the existing `has_objective_backlog` bool (line 325 of core.py)
- `plan`: the plan dict already passed via `QueueBuildOptions`

#### Step 2: Update `WorkQueueResult` and `build_work_queue()`

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`**

Update the TypedDict:

```python
class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    items: list[dict]
    total: int
    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    suppressed_count: int
    new_ids: set[str]          # already present in practice
    phase: str                 # NEW: QueuePhase value
```

In `build_work_queue()`, after building subjective items and before the final return:

```python
# Detect phase
has_unassessed = any(
    item.get("initial_review") for item in all_items
    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
)
phase = detect_queue_phase(
    has_unassessed=has_unassessed,
    has_objective_findings=has_objective_backlog,
    plan=resolved_options.plan,
)

# Gate: in INITIAL_REVIEW phase, filter queue to only show initial_review items
if phase == QueuePhase.INITIAL_REVIEW:
    initial_items = [
        item for item in all_items
        if item.get("initial_review")
        or item.get("kind") == "cluster" and item.get("id") == "auto/initial-review"
    ]
    if initial_items:
        all_items = initial_items
```

This filtering happens **after** the plan ordering and cluster collapsing, so if the `auto/initial-review` cluster is present, it gets shown. If not (no plan yet), the individual `initial_review=True` items show through.

The return dict gets the new field:

```python
return {
    "items": all_items,
    "total": total,
    "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
    "new_ids": new_ids,
    "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
    "phase": phase.value,
}
```

Update `__all__` to export `QueuePhase` and `detect_queue_phase`.

#### Step 4: Render phase-specific banners in `next` command

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`**

Add a new function `render_phase_banner()`:

```python
def render_phase_banner(phase: str, queue_total: int) -> bool:
    """Render an onboarding phase banner above the queue. Returns True if shown."""
    if phase == "initial_review":
        print(colorize(
            "\n  INITIAL REVIEW — complete subjective assessments before seeing the full queue.",
            "yellow",
        ))
        print(colorize(
            "  These dimensions need your human judgment to calibrate the scoring model.",
            "dim",
        ))
        return True
    
    if phase == "plan_needed":
        print(colorize(
            "\n  Initial reviews complete. Run `desloppify plan` to build your prioritized queue.",
            "cyan",
        ))
        print(colorize(
            "  Planning organizes findings into clusters and lets you skip/defer items.",
            "dim",
        ))
        return True
    
    return False
```

```python
def format_queue_block(
    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
    *,
    frozen_score: float | None = None,
) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    
    # Phase banner (takes priority over other contextual hints)
    if breakdown.phase == "initial_review":
        lines.append((
            "  Complete initial reviews to unlock the full queue.",
            "yellow",
        ))
    elif breakdown.phase == "plan_needed":
        lines.append((
            "  Run `desloppify plan` to organize your queue.",
            "cyan",
        ))
    
    # ... existing focus banner, frozen score, queue headline, etc. ...
```

Test the detection function directly:

```python
def test_detect_phase_initial_review():
    phase = detect_queue_phase(
        has_unassessed=True,
        has_objective_findings=True,
        plan={"queue_order": ["subjective::test"]},
    )
    assert phase == QueuePhase.INITIAL_REVIEW

def test_detect_phase_plan_needed():
    phase = detect_queue_phase(
        has_unassessed=False,
        has_objective_findings=True,
        plan={"queue_order": ["subjective::done_dim"], "plan_start_scores": {}},
    )
    assert phase == QueuePhase.PLAN_NEEDED

def test_detect_phase_working():
    phase = detect_queue_phase(
        has_unassessed=False,
        has_objective_findings=True,
        plan={
            "queue_order": ["finding::some_id"],
            "plan_start_scores": {"strict": 85.0},
        },
    )
    assert phase == QueuePhase.WORKING
```

### Potential Challenges

1. **Backward compatibility of `WorkQueueResult`**: Since it is a TypedDict, adding a new key is safe -- callers using `.get("phase", "working")` will get a default. All existing code that destructures the result via dict access continues to work.

2. **Interaction with cluster collapsing**: When in `INITIAL_REVIEW` phase, the `auto/initial-review` cluster might be the collapsed form of the individual initial review items. The filter needs to accept both the cluster and individual items. The check `item.get("id") == "auto/initial-review"` handles the cluster case, and `item.get("initial_review")` handles uncollapsed items.

3. **Interaction with `scope`/`cluster` filtering**: If the user explicitly passes `--scope` or `--cluster`, the phase gate should NOT override their explicit filter. Add a guard: only apply the INITIAL_REVIEW filter when `scope` and `cluster` are both None.

4. **Phase detection when no plan exists**: If `plan` is `None` (no plan file), we cannot determine `PLAN_NEEDED` because we have no `queue_order` to inspect. When `plan` is `None` and there are unassessed dims, it is `INITIAL_REVIEW`. When `plan` is `None` and no unassessed but objective findings exist, it should be `PLAN_NEEDED` (the user has never created a plan). Adjust the detection:

```python
if has_objective_findings and not has_unassessed:
    if plan is None:
        return QueuePhase.PLAN_NEEDED
    # plan exists but is empty
    has_plan_content = any(...)
    has_plan_cycle = bool(plan.get("plan_start_scores"))
    if not has_plan_content and not has_plan_cycle:
        return QueuePhase.PLAN_NEEDED
```

5. **Rendering consistency**: The `status` command computes its own `_breakdown` via `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()`. Since we are adding `phase` to `QueueBreakdown`, it will be available there. But `status` also builds its own subjective display logic (`show_subjective_followup`, `show_dimension_table`). We should make sure the `INITIAL_REVIEW` phase banner does not duplicate with those existing displays.

### Dependency Ordering

1. `QueuePhase` enum and `detect_queue_phase()` -- no dependencies
2. Update `WorkQueueResult` TypedDict and `build_work_queue()` -- depends on step 1
3. Update `QueueBreakdown` -- depends on step 2  
4. Render functions -- depends on steps 2-3
5. Command modules -- depends on step 4
6. Output/payload -- depends on step 2
7. Tests -- depends on all above

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` - Core location for QueuePhase enum, detect_queue_phase() function, and the INITIAL_REVIEW gate in build_work_queue()
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py` - QueueBreakdown dataclass needs the phase field; format_queue_block() gets phase-aware hints
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` - New render_phase_banner() function and integration into the rendering pipeline
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py` - Wire phase through _get_items() to control banner rendering before queue items
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py` - Phase-aware banners in the status dashboard
